### [1] [Three-mode chimera-like states in a minimal network of active camphor ribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26039)
*Ratan Sarkar,Sapna Yadav,Punit Parmananda,Charo I. del Genio,Stefano Boccaletti*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在四个相同樟脑椭圆转子（方形底座四角枢转、水面Marangoni流驱动）系统中，实验观测到同步、反同步与非相干三种集体态并存的三模式类chimera态，验证了反应-扩散模型中三域chimera的理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chimera态是相同单元自发分裂为相干与非相干群体的对称破缺现象；近期理论在反应-扩散模型中提出"三域chimera"，但缺乏实验验证，需要可在受控物理平台上实现并观测该新类型chimera态的系统。

Method: 构建由四个完全相同的樟脑浸润椭圆转子组成的实验系统，每个转子枢转于方形底座的角点；置于水面时，樟脑溶解产生的表面张力梯度（Marangoni流）驱动转子自推进与转动；转子通过共享的水-气界面形成化学耦合（樟脑浓度场），从而产生集体动力学。实验记录并分类集体状态。

Result: 系统呈现三种不同的集体动力学状态：同步态、反同步态与非相干态，并在同一系统中并存，构成三模式（chimera-like）态。实验结果与关于反应-扩散模型中三域chimera的近期理论预言一致。

Conclusion: 实验首次（据作者所述）证实了三模式类chimera态的存在，为三域chimera理论提供实证支持，深化了对非线性动力学与对称破缺的理解，并表明此类新型chimera态可在一大类物理系统中被观测。

Abstract: Chimera states are a remarkable manifestation of symmetry breaking, wherein identical units spontaneously split into coherent and incoherent groups. The term "chimera" itself is inspired by the mythological creature having parts of different animals, reflecting the coexistence of distinct dynamical behaviors within a single system. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the existence of a three-mode chimera-like state in a system of four identical camphor-infused elliptical rotors, each pivoted at a corner of a square base. When placed on a water surface, the rotors self-propel and rotate due to Marangoni-driven flows. Chemical coupling mediated through the shared water interface gives rise to three distinct collective dynamical states, namely a synchronized one, an antisynchronized one and an incoherent one. Our results validate recent theoretical work about three-domain chimeras in reaction-diffusion models, providing new insights into nonlinear dynamics and suggesting that this new type of chimera may be observed in a broad class of physical systems.

</details>


### [2] [Onset of subdiffusion in dephased quasiperiodic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26082)
*Ashlin V. Thomas,Sanchayan Banerjee,Tapan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期晶格在退相位下的输运行为，证明当对角势奇异或跃迁为零（本应导致平凡局域化）时，输运可呈亚扩散，且存在由调制强度决定的扩散-亚扩散转变，输运最慢处恰在亚扩散起始点形成"亚扩散沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍预期退相位破坏相位相干性，从而在准周期晶格中诱导扩散。但作者指出这一预期在含奇异在位势或零跃迁的晶格中可能严重失效；此类晶格本应平凡地局域化粒子，其实际输运规律尚不明确，需要重新审视。

Method: 结合解析分析与数值计算，考察准周期调制强度变化下的输运，引入晶格"平均电阻"发散的判据；并辅以精确的 Lindblad 主方程模拟，同时提出可能的实验实现途径。

Result: 当晶格"平均电阻"发散且条件适当时，输运呈亚扩散；随准周期调制强度变化出现从扩散到亚扩散的转变；输运最慢的位置不在亚扩散区深处，而恰在转变起始处，形成尖锐的"亚扩散沟"；输运指数具有普适性，仅由对角（非对角）准周期调制的奇异性（零点）阶数决定。

Conclusion: 退相位并不必然导致准周期晶格中的扩散；奇异在位势或零跃迁体系可呈现由奇异性阶数唯一确定的普适亚扩散输运，并可通过 Lindblad 模拟与实验方案验证。

Abstract: Dephasing is widely expected to induce diffusion in quasiperiodic lattices due to the loss of phase coherence. However, this expectation can fail dramatically in lattices possessing singular onsite potentials or vanishing hoppings which are expected to trivially localize the particle. Remarkably, we show that the transport in such lattices can be subdiffusive under proper conditions for which the "mean resistance" of the lattice diverges. As a result, we obtain a transition from diffusive to subdiffusive transport depending on the strength of quasiperiodic modulation. Even more strikingly, the transport is slowest not deep in the subdiffusive regime but precisely at its onset, forming a sharp "subdiffusive trench". Our analytical and numerical analysis reveal that the transport exponents are universal, fixed solely by the order of singularities (zeros) of the diagonal (off-diagonal) quasiperiodic modulation. In the end, we complement our findings through exact Lindblad simulations and provide possible experimental routes to realize such non-trivial transport.

</details>

### [3] [Chern Numbers from Symmetry-Enforced Zeros and Dispersion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25028)
*Yoonseok Hwang,Henry Davenport,Frank Schindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用Chern带与自由选取参考态之间的波函数交叠，证明晶格对称性可强制交叠零点并固定其涡旋度（模旋转阶数），且弱局域微扰产生的能带色散移动正比于交叠模平方，从而无需测量波函数相位即可识别Chern拓扑，并给出量子度量的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Chern拓扑刻画依赖Berry曲率，需要对波函数相位进行测量或计算，实验与数值上都较为繁琐。作者希望寻找一种互补的表征方式：利用波函数零点的位置与局部涡旋度来刻画Chern数，并使之可以通过可直接观测的能带色散读出，从而避免相位测量。

Method: 以Chern带波函数与任意自由选取的参考态构造动量空间交叠函数；借助晶格（晶体）对称性分析交叠零点的位置约束，并根据能带与参考态的角动量确定零点涡旋度（以旋转阶数为模）。进一步引入弱局域微扰，推导能带色散移动正比于交叠模平方的关系，使零点和局部结构可从色散中直接读出。构造适用于多分量、多带波函数，并允许参考态在动量空间表示于某些动量处消失（如紧局域态）。

Result: 证明晶体对称性可强制交叠零点并对涡旋度给出确定约束；弱局域微扰导致的色散移动与交叠模平方成正比，因此零点和局部结构可由色散直接读取，结合对称性即可识别Chern拓扑而无需测量波函数相位；交叠零点附近的色散移动还给出该点量子度量的下界。

Conclusion: 波函数零点及其局部涡旋度为Chern拓扑提供了与Berry曲率互补且更易观测的刻画；该方法适用于多分量/多带体系及紧局域参考态，可用于平带系统中利用其已知的实空间与动量空间性质，并为量子度量提供下界估计。

Abstract: Wave-function zeros and their local vorticities provide a complementary characterization of Chern topology to more commonly used Berry-curvature formulations. Using wave-function overlaps between a Chern band and freely chosen reference states, we show that crystalline symmetry can enforce their zeros and fix their vorticities modulo the rotation order from the angular momenta of the band and reference state. We further show that weak local perturbations produce band-dispersion shifts proportional to the absolute square of the overlap, allowing its zeros and local structure to be read off directly from the dispersion. Combined with crystalline symmetry, this can identify Chern topology without measuring wave-function phases. Our construction applies to multicomponent and multiband wave functions and allows reference states whose momentum-space representation may vanish at some momenta. This permits compact localized states to be used directly as reference states and makes use of their known real- and momentum-space properties in flat-band systems. The dispersion shift near an overlap zero further provides a lower bound on the quantum metric at the zero.

</details>


### [4] [Circuit model for gate-induced excitation of quantum Hall edges tuned by DC gate bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25332)
*Kodai Sugizaki,Takaaki Mano,Go Yusa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究栅极诱导边缘激发的输入电压与激发波形关系，发现随栅压偏移变化，波形从类一阶微分响应转变为与输入成正比，并可用单一等效电路模型（捏断电压两侧不同有效栅-2DEG电容）统一描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘激发在低维电子系统与介观输运研究中有重要意义，但栅极诱导激发的输入电压与激发波形之间的定量关系尚不清楚，需要建立可解释的等效电路描述。

Method: 通过改变施加于栅极的偏置电压，在时域与频域分别测量栅极诱导激发的响应波形；构建含有效栅-2DEG电容的等效电路模型对数据拟合，并与由器件结构计算得到的电容进行对比验证。

Result: 时域上，激发波形随偏置电压由类一阶微分响应过渡为与输入波形成正比的响应；频域上，栅极激发的频率依赖在偏置电压跨越捏断电压时发生显著变化。该变化可由单一等效电路模型描述，仅需在捏断电压两侧取不同的有效栅-2DEG电容；拟合得到的有效电容与器件结构计算值一致。

Conclusion: 栅极诱导边缘激发的波形演化可由捏断电压两侧有效栅电容不同的单一等效电路模型统一解释，且拟合电容与器件几何计算结果吻合，验证了模型的物理合理性。

Abstract: Edge excitations, which propagate along the edge of a quantum Hall system, are of great interest in the study of low-dimensional electron systems and mesoscopic transport phenomena. In this study, we investigate the relationship between the input voltage and the excitation waveform for gate-induced excitations. By varying the offset voltage applied to the gate, we observe that the excitation waveform changes from a first-derivative-like response to a response proportional to the input waveform in the time domain. In the frequency domain, we observe that the frequency dependence of the gate excitation changes significantly as the offset voltage crosses the pinch-off voltage. We show that these changes can be described by a single equivalent circuit model with different effective gate-2DEG capacitances on either side of the pinch-off voltage. In addition, we confirm that the effective capacitance obtained from the fitting of the model with the experimental data is consistent with the capacitance calculated from the device structure.

</details>


### [5] [Local orbital magnetization and Chern markers from equilibrium local currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26599)
*Henrique P. Veiga,Aires Ferreira,Caio H. Lewenkopf,João M. Viana Parente Lopes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于滤波随机向量的半正定算符矩阵元一次性估计方法，并经由离散泊松方程将键电流转为环电流，得到局域轨道磁化与一种无位置算符、无边界伪影的新局域Chern marker。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域电流承载宏观样品的轨道磁化，是建模非均匀磁性材料及理解磁电、拓扑响应的关键；但非均匀体系中局域电流的真实感模拟成像仍困难，且此前计算非对角矩阵元需大量独立模拟、效率低。

Method: 在紧束缚模型的随机估计方法基础上，用单组滤波随机向量同时获取格点上半正定算符的全部矩阵元；将键电流表示为满足离散泊松方程的环电流，以电流涡度为源项，求解得到局域轨道磁化；再类比全局Středa关系，对该磁化关于化学势求导定义新的局域Chern marker。

Result: 单组滤波随机向量即可同时获得半正定算符所有矩阵元，免去非对角元的独立模拟，显著加速局域电流空间图的计算；新局域Chern marker完全由键电流构造，无需位置算符，不受开放样品投影marker零和规则限制，因而无边界补偿伪影，其样品平均值以受控方式收敛到体Chern数。

Conclusion: 随机估计与泊松方程求解相结合，可高效生成非均匀体系局域电流图像，并给出物理意义清晰（Středa关系类比）、数值上鲁棒（无边界伪影、样本平均收敛至体拓扑不变量）的局域拓扑表征工具。

Abstract: Local currents carry the orbital magnetization of a macroscopic sample and are key for modeling inhomogeneous magnetic materials and for understanding magnetoelectric and topological responses. However, producing realistic simulated images of local currents in inhomogeneous systems remains challenging. Building upon recent work on stochastic estimation methods for tight-binding models, we show that all matrix elements of positive semidefinite operators defined on a lattice can be obtained at once from a single set of filtered random vectors. This eliminates the need for independent simulations previously required for off-diagonal elements, which significantly speeds up the calculation of spatial maps of local currents. Further, we express bond currents in terms of loop currents that obey a discrete Poisson equation, with the current vorticity acting as the source term. Its solution yields a local orbital magnetization. In analogy with the global Středa relation, the derivative of this magnetization with respect to the chemical potential defines a new local Chern marker. Built entirely from bond currents, it requires no position operator and is not bound by the zero-sum rule of projector markers in open samples. Hence, it is free of boundary-compensation artifacts, and its sample average converges to the bulk Chern number in a controlled fashion.

</details>


### [6] [Topological Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25493)
*Dan-Na Liu,J. Villavicencio,Pierre A. Pantaleon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文在AB堆叠双层石墨烯的质量墙结中发现拓扑连续谱束缚态（BIC）：谷拓扑保证界面态沿墙传播，而束缚条件的另一种绕数决定其与导电引线的精确解耦；调节结长或层间偏压可使带相反电荷的束缚态合并并解除束缚，而谷拓扑不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑界面态通常与导电连续谱耦合而展宽为共振，如何在导电连续谱中实现精确束缚（BIC）并与界面传播拓扑相区分，是拓扑束缚态物理的核心问题；双层石墨烯质量墙结提供了一个可同时容纳两种拓扑角色的平台。

Method: 研究AB堆叠双层石墨烯中的质量墙结（mass-wall junction）构型，分析其谷拓扑所保证的界面态，并引入描述束缚条件的独立绕数（winding number）刻画与外部导电引线的解耦；通过连续模型/散射分析考察结长与层间偏压对能谱的调控，以及临界点附近共振宽度的标度行为。

Result: 发现沿墙传播的谷拓扑界面态可与连续谱精确解耦形成拓扑BIC；调节结长或层间偏压可使带相反电荷的束缚态相互合并并解除束缚，而谷拓扑结构保持不变；在一般临界点处，束缚态仍保持束缚，且邻近共振宽度随动量的四次方（quartically）开启。

Conclusion: 拓扑在界面传播与连续谱中束缚两方面扮演互补角色，二者由不同拓扑量分别控制；该结论为可调、可解耦的拓扑束缚态及其临界行为提供了明确判据，并拓展了BIC与谷拓扑物理的联系。

Abstract: We uncover topological bound states in the continuum in AB-stacked bilayer graphene mass-wall junctions. Valley topology supports interface states propagating along the wall, while a distinct winding of the confinement conditions governs exact decoupling from conducting leads. Tuning the junction length or interlayer bias can merge oppositely charged bound states and remove confinement without changing the valley topology. At generic critical points, the state remains bound and nearby resonance widths open quartically with momentum. These results connect complementary roles of topology in interface propagation and confinement within a conducting continuum.

</details>


### [7] [Field-free deterministic spin-orbit torque magnetization switching using monolayer WTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25755)
*Ganesh Hanchanahal,Arnav Jain,Shrushti Tapar,Saroj P Dash,Bhaskaran Muralidharan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以单层WTe₂为自旋源，比较1T_d与1T'两相的对称性、电流诱导自旋极化与无场确定性翻转性能，指出x-ribbon 1T_d WTe₂是SOT-MRAM的优选自旋源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自旋轨道矩磁存储（SOT-MRAM）的关键一步是磁化矢量的无外场翻转；需要寻找无需外加磁场、能提供确定性翻转的自旋源材料，并明确其相结构与几何参数对翻转动力学的影响。

Method: 构建以单层WTe₂（1T_d与1T'相）为自旋源的SOT翻转器件，对两相（均具备无场确定性翻转所需对称性）进行对比分析；将铁磁层建模为交换耦合的宏自旋，在非平衡格林函数（NEGF）框架内计算电流诱导自旋极化；并系统改变偏压、ribbon取向与磁层宽度，研究翻转动力学。

Result: 两相体系均实现翻转；1T_d相的x-ribbon几何产生显著更大的电流诱导自旋极化，从而在相邻磁层上产生更大的类阻尼矩（damping-like torque）并实现更快翻转。1T'器件的翻转时间随磁层宽度呈强烈且非单调变化；1T_d器件在较宽宽度范围内翻转时间近乎恒定，与边缘主导的力矩机制一致。

Conclusion: x-ribbon 1T_d WTe₂在自旋极化强度、力矩效率与翻转时间对宽度的鲁棒性上优于1T'相，是无需外场SOT-MRAM的有前景自旋源材料。

Abstract: An important step towards the practical implementation of spin-orbit torque magnetic memory is the field-free switching of the magnetization vector. In this work, we investigate spin-orbit torque-driven magnetization dynamics and switching devices comprising monolayer $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as the spin-source material. We perform a comparative analysis of the two phases of $\mathrm{WTe}_2$, namely the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ and $1\mathrm{T}'$ with the necessary symmetries for field-free deterministic magnetization switching. We demonstrate switching in both systems and analyse the dependence of the switching dynamics on the applied bias voltage, ribbon orientation and width of the magnetic layer. Modelling the ferromagnet as an exchange-coupled macrospin within a non-equilibrium Green's function framework, we find that the $x$-ribbon geometry of the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ phase generates a significantly larger current-induced spin polarization than the $1\mathrm{T}'$ phase, resulting in a larger damping-like torque on the adjacent magnetic layer and faster switching. The switching time of $1\mathrm{T}'$ devices varies strongly and non-monotonically with the width of the magnetic layer. In $1\mathrm{T}_d$ devices, it remains nearly constant over a broad range of widths, consistent with an edge-dominated torque. These results identify $x$-ribbon $1\mathrm{T}_d$-$\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as a promising spin source for field-free SOT-MRAM.

</details>


### [8] [Helices at Twisted Ferroelectric Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25828)
*Kristina Holsgrove,Ronan Lynch,Conor McCluskey,Eoin Moynihan,Andrew Rogers,Xinxin Hu,Boris Croes,Loan Hajdari-Manigault,Maria Lazarut,Eadaoin Anderson,Sam Seddon,Anna Razumnaya,Yurii Tikhonov,Ana M. Sanchez,Marios Hadjimichael,Amit Kumar,Raymond McQuaid,Jordi Arbiol,Gustau Catalan,Richard Beanland,Igor Lukyanchuk,Lukas M. Eng,Salia Cherifi-Hertel,Tchavdar Todorov,J. Marty Gregg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在扭转的铁电铌酸锂单晶界面中发现了由螺位错分隔、相干键合且弹性变形的纳米尺度结构螺旋阵列；该螺旋同时诱导形成偶极螺旋，具有手性，可由4D STEM/DPC、电子叠层成像与SHG圆二色测量证实，构成区别于莫尔条纹的新型"twistronics"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有twistronics主要基于二维范德华材料的扭转堆叠（莫尔超晶格），而强键合的氧化物扭转界面是否产生不同物理尚不清楚；作者希望探究单晶铁电氧化物扭转界面的真实原子结构与由此衍生的极性/手性响应。

Result: 界面处存在阵列式纳米结构螺旋，彼此由螺位错分隔；该类螺旋在4D STEM/DPC中产生显著衬度并可由电子通道轨迹模型定量解释，并被电子叠层成像直接观测；扭转应变经压电/挠曲电效应必然产生偶极螺旋；翻转实验与SHG-CPD显示异常大的界面手性信号，证实螺旋的手性特征。

Conclusion: 所观察的螺旋阵列不同于莫尔图案：它是兼具塑性（位错）与弹性变形的晶格旋转结构，而非两套晶格的简单叠加，拓扑上区别于常见的涡旋且具有手性。因此强键合的扭转氧化物界面与二维范德华扭转堆叠性质本质不同，开辟了一类新的twistronics方向。

Abstract: We report that twisted interfaces, formed between two ferroelectric lithium niobate single crystals, contain arrays of coherently bonded and elastically deformed nanoscale structural helices, separated from one another by screw dislocations. The helices generate remarkable contrast in four dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D STEM) and through related differential phase contrast (DPC). Such images have been successfully rationalised by modelling the trajectory of electrons that channel along helical pathways as they traverse the interface, but the structural helices implied have also been directly imaged using electron ptychography. Importantly, as lithium niobate is a uniaxial ferroelectric, the torsional strain in these structural helices necessarily also causes dipolar helices to form, inferred through consideration of pertinent piezoelectric tensors and flexoelectric coefficients. Helices are topologically distinct from the more commonly reported vortices and, importantly, they are chiral. Helical (and hence chiral) character was confirmed by observing that DPC images were identical before and after the sample was inverted. Chirality was also established through second harmonic optical circularly-polarised dichroism measurements, in which unusually large interfacial signals were seen. It must be stressed that the arrays of helices observed are distinct from moiré patterns; they facilitate lattice rotation combining plastic and elastic deformation and are not simply a superposition of lattices, twisted with respect to one another. Thus, intimately bonded twisted oxide interfaces will have distinctly different characteristics from those of twisted stacks of two-dimensional van der Waals materials, opening up a new and distinct kind of twistronics.

</details>

Abstract: Chiral magnets host hopfions, but their experimentally observed configurations are largely limited to hopfion rings, heliknotons, and their clusters. This criterion essentially bounds the likelihood of hidden localized defects arising from quantum Hall breakdown and has potential uses in quantum computing and semiconductor testing but is presented in the context of its application toward quantum electrical standards.

Abstract: Thermal boundary conductance (TBC) plays a crucial role in heat dissipation in two-dimensional (2D) devices. However, reported values for 2D material classes such as monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) can vary by more than an order of magnitude across the literature, making it difficult to determine whether these differences come from the materials themselves or from the measurement methods used. To address this issue, this article presents a systematic, cross-technique study of the interfacial heat transport in four TMD monolayers supported on a sapphire substrate, using three of the most representative methods: Raman thermometry, scanning thermal microscopy (SThM) and frequency domain thermoreflectance (FDTR). By conducting all measurements on the same sets of samples, the influence of sample-to-sample variation is minimized allowing for a direct comparison of the three approaches. The results reveal a variation in the measured values depending on both the material and the measurement technique. By directly comparing these results we can elucidate on the origin of the differences and underpin sources of uncertainty in each of the characterisation methods. Taken together, these results provide a direct experimental benchmark for studies of 2D material interfaces and offer practical guidance for the selection and interpretation of thermal metrology.

Abstract: A fundamental property of interlayer excitons is their dipolar character, enabling electrical tunability, enhanced exciton-exciton interactions and extended lifetime. It is important for studying collective phenomena and realizing novel optoelectronic devices. However, in homo- or heterostructures based on two-dimensional semiconductors, the magnitude and orientation of the dipole moment are commonly locked due to spatially confined charges and nature of the band transition. Here, we use gate-dependent photoluminescence of few-layer R-WSe2 to harness sliding ferroelectricity for controlling the exciton dipole configuration of emerging layer-hybridised excitons. The sliding-ferroelectric control enables a pronounced on/off switching of the diffusive transport of hybrid dipolar excitons, while further facilitating gradual and abrupt dipole reconfigurations with emerging quadrupolar excitons. Our optical probe reveals intermediate states of vertically aligned interfacial polarisation with magnitudes that confirm cumulative polarisation in multilayers. Overall, our work presents sliding ferroelectricity as a powerful tool to tune interlayer coupling and layer hybridisation. These results represent a step toward ferroelectric-controlled exciton interconnects and novel multi-bit optical memories.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑无能隙模式（能带节点、拓扑绝缘体边界态等）分散于多个体系，各自对应不同的拓扑分类与不变量，缺乏统一描述框架。

Abstract: Topologically protected gapless modes emerge across multiple platforms, ranging from nodes in the band structure to boundary states in topologically non-trivial insulators, with each case governed by distinct topological classifications and invariants. Here, we generalize the spectral localizer method to establish a unified framework for topological gapless modes. The spectral localizer reduces the original Hamiltonian to a zero-dimensional operator by recursively applying quantum confining operators, which need not be diagonal in either position or momentum space, as previously assumed. In phase space, the zero-manifold of the semiclassical confining operators forms a shell enclosing the gapless mode of interest, analogous to the Teo-Kane framework for topological defects. The localizer yields a zero-dimensional invariant, such as a signature, a determinant sign, or a Pfaffian sign, capturing both $\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ classifications. This single construction thus encompasses diverse scenarios, including hinge modes in higher-order topological insulators, individual Dirac nodes in semimetals, and valley-selective topological boundary modes. We illustrate our approach using lattice models in symmetry classes A, AIII, AII, and D, verifying that topological transitions coincide with a vanishing gap in the spectral localizer operator.

</details>

Abstract: The role of disorder in orbital transport remains a central open question in orbitronics. Using a real-space formulation of itinerant orbital angular momentum, we investigate the real-time or- bital Hall transport in single-orbital square-lattice and graphene-based systems. We demonstrate that both structural (polycrystalline and amorphous) and electrostatic disorder can generate siz- able orbital Hall currents that are absent in their pristine counterparts. Remarkably, in amorphous graphene, these currents persist over extended timescales and across a broad energy window around charge neutrality, despite the strong localization of electronic states. Consistent with recent exper- imental evidence, our findings establish amorphous quantum materials as promising platforms for disorder-engineered orbitronic functionality.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相反的能带反转在霍尔响应中相消，其边界模式可被开隙，但缺乏对能够"幸存"于这种相消的拓扑的体探测手段。

Abstract: Band inversions with opposite Chern numbers cancel in the Hall response and yield counterpropagating boundary modes that can gap out. However, a bulk probe of the topology that survives such a cancellation is lacking. We here show that dislocations in inversion-symmetric two-band crystals with cancelling band inversions bind a half-integer charge, which reveals the quantized bulk polarization of the bond-centered obstructed atomic limit realized by such crystals. To demonstrate this, we continuously deform a broad class of two-band lattice models into decoupled chains, without closing the bulk gap or breaking the inversion symmetry, where the dislocation charge follows from a count of occupied bonds. Notably, the fractional part of this charge is insensitive to local perturbations of the dislocation core, and it therefore persists after the in-gap core state is removed, as we show analytically in the chain limit and numerically away from it. In the Weyl-semimetal counterpart, the response to a magnetic flux separates this crystalline charge from the charge set by the Chern number of each momentum slice. We propose that a half-integer core charge that persists after the in-gap peak disappears from the local spectrum can be detected in artificial electronic and optical lattices, with metamaterials providing complementary access to the core modes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QPE的相位精度受教育于相位量子比特数所导致的数字化误差限制，而增加量子比特数会加深电路深度，不利于近期量子设备的实用化。因此需要一种在浅层电路下仍能提升本征值（尤其最小本征值）计算精度的方案。

Method: 在QPE量子电路中引入优化循环，将哈密顿量的演化时间作为可学习参数，以测量所得本征值的相位分布的香农熵为目标函数进行最小化；其依据是标准QPE中相位分布熵与最小本征值相位的模拟（连续）精度之间存在自然对应关系。

Result: 在量子磁模型上的数值实验表明，最小本征值的计算精度得到显著提升；且该方法对相位量子比特数不敏感，可在较短的QPE电路深度下实现更高精度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将参数学习引入QPE框架，通过熵最小化控制演化时间，突破相位数字化误差限制，使QPE在基础科学研究中的近期实用性更为可行。

Abstract: Learning a Hamiltonian evolution time in Quantum Phase Estimation that produces phase distributions with low Shannon entropy circumvents the fundamental limit of digitization error of phase, independent of the number of phase qubits of accuracy. By introducing an optimization loop to minimize the Shannon entropy of measured eigenvalues produced by a QPE quantum circuit, minimum eigenvalue computation accuracy is greatly improved, which we show numerically on quantum magnet models. This technique works because standard QPE has a natural correspondence between entropy of the phase distribution and the analog accuracy of the minimum eigenvalue phase. This introduces for the first time a type of parameter learning to QPE, which makes near term practicality of QPE for fundamental sciences more feasible by using shorter depth QPE circuits.

</details>


### [17] [Entanglement Geometry Beyond Polygons: Graph-Theoretic Entanglement Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25019)
*Masoud Gharahi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Can a machine provably discover new quantum phases of matter? We show that the answer is yes under physically motivated assumptions. The algorithm takes as input only a classical description of the family together with unverified preparations of reference ground states; no order parameter, symmetry-breaking pattern, or training data are supplied. It succeeds provided that at least one preparation is correct in each gapped region of parameter space. The algorithm explores parameter space by quasi-adiabatically or adiabatically transporting each reference state, detecting the approach of a phase boundary by tapping the transported state with a frequency-filtered local probe: excitations created by the probe witness the closing of the energy gap. The gap-detecting measurement is gentle, so when the ground state is unique a single reusable copy of each reference ground state supports the full exploration (for degenerate ground states, polynomially many copies suffice). The quantum algorithm runs in polynomial time at inverse-polynomial gap resolution. We also give a classical algorithm that runs in quasipolynomial time at constant gap resolution. Result: 证明qudit模型与febit模型在自检验中不可互换（存在自检验qudit策略唯一而费米子实现不唯一的关联）；并在三方重连网络中证明其关联可将febit态与测量在局域宇称保持等距意义下唯一确定，实现首个真正的费米子自检验。

We first show that the modeling choice in terms of qudits or febits matters for self-testing: some correlations that self-test a unique qudit strategy admit two fermionic realizations inequivalent under local parity-preserving isometries. We then develop a fermionic self-testing framework and show that the correlations of a three-party rewiring network single out febit states and measurements up to such isometries, providing the first genuine fermionic self-test.

Abstract: Quantum frameworks each define their own Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) dialect, so porting a circuit to a Quantum Intermediate Representation (QIR) runtime needs a hand-written pass per dialect. A dual-backend pipeline executes the source MLIR and generated QIR on independent simulators, comparing output distributions by total variation distance (TVD). For smaller open-weight models we replace retrieval-augmented generation with inline context engineering and add a guardrail-assembler that rebuilds valid QIR from incomplete output. Across 41 circuits spanning three dialects and 1-100 qubits, the deterministic path is gate-exact on all 70 circuit-dialect pairs, averaging above 95% TVD similarity (1-TVD). On an unseen fault-tolerant (FTQC) dialect, four of six models produce valid logical-level QIR on the first iteration.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求逻辑态制备中单点故障不能扩散为不可纠正错误，后选择（post-selection）通过附加校验检测危险故障并丢弃该次运行来实现这一目标。但校验如何测量决定了电路的深度与操作数，最佳权衡取决于硬件。现有自动化方法各自只采用一种校验构造，因而只能给出固定的权衡点，缺乏可调的设计空间。

Method: 1) 提出 Block Checks：多个校验共享一个可容错制备的辅助块（auxiliary block），将新构造与已有构造统一到同一设计空间；2) 提出 Pareto 树搜索（Pareto tree search），通过添加校验与辅助块、合并辅助块、编辑电路等动作在该空间中探索，从而获得多组帕累托最优（操作数—深度）权衡解。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing needs logical states prepared such that single faults cannot spread into uncorrectable errors. Post-selection achieves this with added checks that detect dangerous faults and discard the run. How the checks are measured sets the depth and operation count of the circuit, and the best trade-off depends on the hardware. Existing automated methods, however, each use one check construction and yield fixed trade-offs.

Second, to explore that space, we propose a Pareto tree search that adds checks and auxiliary blocks, merges auxiliaries, and edits the circuit. On quantum codes of distance up to seven, the search finds best-known circuits for many new operation--depth trade-offs and surpasses existing methods. The code is open source as part of the Munich Quantum Toolkit (MQT) at https://github.com/munich-quantum-toolkit/qecc, and all circuits are available in the QECirc library at https://qecirc.com.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典连续小波变换搬上量子线路时，尺度网格常需非二进、且小波系数为复数，涉及成功概率、资源开销与测量统计等问题；作者希望厘清QCWT相对简单频域测量究竟带来何种"量子增益"，并给出可证实/可界定的性能刻画。

Method: 在有限尺度-平移网格（尺度可非二进）上构造QCWT：QFT → 单flag量子比特块编码的尺度相关乘子 → 逆QFT，成功分支输出正交加权复系数。对"一次作用于单份输入、输出因子与输入无关"的heralded电路推导成功概率上界，并用频率条件化尺度制备达到该界；分析尺度先验与频率独立时逐尺度归一化乘子的最优性；对可逆Mexican-hat乘子做定点资源估计，给出尺度-平移测量的多项分布律与复系数的Hadamard检验；进而构造"傅里叶基测量+经典随机化"的等价采样器，并用Hurst指数估计仿真验证。

Abstract: We construct a quantum continuous wavelet transform (QCWT) on a finite scale--translation grid whose scales may be non-dyadic. A quantum Fourier transform places the amplitude-encoded signal in the frequency basis, the scale-dependent multiplier is block-encoded with one flag qubit, and an inverse Fourier transform returns the complex, quadrature-weighted CWT coefficients in the success branch. For heralded circuits that act once on one copy of the input with an input-independent output factor, we bound the success probability and attain the bound by a frequency-conditioned scale preparation. When the scale prior is prepared independently of frequency, normalizing the multiplier separately at each scale is optimal and raises the success probability by factors of 4.9 and 14.6 in our examples; on logarithmic grids this architecture loses, relative to the bound, a factor of about the admissibility constant over the squared spectral peak times the logarithmic scale range. We give a fixed-point resource estimate for a reversible Mexican-hat multiplier, the multinomial law of scale--translation measurements, and Hadamard tests for complex coefficients. We show that a Fourier-basis measurement of the input, followed by classical randomization, reproduces the flag and scale outcomes of every such heralded circuit exactly, so it is at least as informative for global scale energies; a simulation study of Hurst exponent estimation agrees. What the QCWT adds is the joint scale--translation law: two chirps with identical Fourier-basis and time-basis measurement laws have QCWT laws at total-variation distance 0.27.

Abstract: Quantum simulators have become a reality in recent years, with several platforms now remotely accessible through the cloud. These systems give researchers the opportunity to run quantum programs without requiring either a dedicated laboratory or extensive experimental expertise, and, in several cases, they have already enabled the execution of relevant protocols with remarkable results. However, despite the impressive progress in the performance of these machines, they still suffer from various noise sources, some of which are not always completely clear to the end user, which limits their applicability to tasks that are sufficiently robust against noise of various kinds. In this work, we investigate the behavior of Fresnel, a remotely accessible neutral-atom analog quantum simulator provided by Pasqal. We design and perform a series of experiments probing the machine's response to various pulse sequences and experimental settings, and identify the main noise sources affecting its dynamics. Based on these data, we construct an effective model of the system's noisy evolution and use numerical optimization to infer the noise strengths. The resulting model accurately reproduces the simulator's dynamics, showing how effective noise models can be built for cloud-based quantum resources.

</details>

*Ruizhe Qian,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散映射将 PCA 推广到非线性流形，可用于谱降维、几何数据分析与点云上的椭圆 PDE 近似；但对规模为 \(N\) 的数据集，经典矩阵运算需 \(\operatorname{poly}(N)\) 复杂度，即使只提取低维谱特征或特定标量可观测量也难以承受。作者希望绕开显式构造图拉普拉斯矩阵。

Method: 不显式组装图拉普拉斯，而是在块编码层面直接利用对称因子分解 \(G=A^TA\)；组合高斯矩扩张（Gaussian moment dilation）、Laplace 加权酉算子线性组合（LCU）与量子奇异值变换（QSVT）来实现 \(A\) 的谱滤波，用于估计热半群重叠、正则化逆查询与能量响应，以及制备谱 Galerkin 解态。另提出公共态构造，使同一次块编码查询可复用于多个标量可观测量。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模点云上的扩散映射与相关谱任务提供了指数级提速路径，关键在于避免拉普拉斯显式构造、直接在块编码层面使用对称分解，并通过公共态复用降低多次可观测量查询开销；验证目前限于理想态矢量模拟与基准比较。

Abstract: Diffusion maps generalize principal component analysis (PCA) to nonlinear manifolds, enabling spectral dimensionality reduction, geometric data analysis, and elliptic PDE approximation on point clouds. For large datasets of size $N$, however, classical matrix operations incur prohibitive $\operatorname{poly}(N)$ scaling, even when extracting low-dimensional spectral features or targeted scalar observables. We introduce a quantum algorithmic framework that bypasses explicit graph-Laplacian assembly by exploiting the symmetric factorization $G = A^T A$ directly at the block-encoding level. Combining Gaussian moment dilation, Laplace-weighted linear combinations of unitaries (LCU), and quantum singular value transformation (QSVT), we implement spectral filters of $A$ to estimate heat-semigroup overlaps, regularized inverse queries, and energy responses, as well as to prepare spectral Galerkin solution states. A common-state construction further allows the block-encoding queries to be used across multiple scalar observables. Under polylogarithmic-cost block-encoding access to $A$ and state-preparation oracles, our heat-overlap algorithm achieves $O(\operatorname{polylog} N)$ gate complexity at fixed normalized precision, confidence, and spectral scale. Ideal statevector simulations validate our discrete circuit constructions, while a matched approximate-counting benchmark demonstrates the quadratic precision advantage of coherent amplitude estimation for energy readout.

### [32] [Local-channel inequivalence within analytic symmetry-broken phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26139)
*Guanxi Xun,Rintaro Masaoka,Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在单一连续对称破缺相内、自由能解析的情况下，不同温度的Gibbs态在两路准局域信道下仍可互不等价；其判据是一个由相刚度Υ(T)决定的可转换不变量C(T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为同一相内、自由能解析且对称性未变的Gibbs态属"同一物质相"，应可互相转换。作者要检验这一直觉在准局域（有限因果锥、允许两路经典通信）操作框架下是否成立，并寻找刻画有序相内热态可转换性的定量不变量。

The maximum mutual information they can obtain by separate measurements grows logarithmically with the radius of their regions. The leading term is the same throughout each ordered phase, while the constant term $\mathcal{C}(T)=\tfrac12\ln[Υ(T)/T]+c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ is strictly larger at lower temperatures. Here $Υ(T)$ is the phase stiffness and $c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ depends only on the region geometry. A quasi-local channel cannot increase this constant, so it is invariant under two-way conversion. Cooling would increase it and is therefore impossible between any two distinct temperatures in the ordered phase. On finite lattices, cooling with vanishing error requires a circuit whose causal radius grows at least linearly with the lattice side length. Abstract: Experimental demonstrations of quantum error correction often exhibit noise that varies across space and time, and attempting to characterize this disorder has been shown to substantially improve decoding performance. We show that the decoding performance is set by a small number of information-theoretic functionals of the noise distribution: the entropy determines the threshold while Bhattacharyya coefficients bound the deep sub-threshold decay rate of logical failures. Furthermore, our results give a quantitative measure of the benefits of characterizing this noise. We numerically investigate both maximum-likelihood and minimum-weight-error decoding of surface codes, showing good agreement with theory across the code-capacity, phenomenological, and circuit-level noise settings, including with noise distributions seen in open-access superconducting devices. Abstract: In classical Hamiltonian systems with a slowly time-dependent parameter, adiabaticity breaks down at a separatrix, leading to probabilistic post-adiabatic transitions between different phase-space regions described by the Kruskal-Neishtadt-Henrard (KNH) theorem. Abstract: Computational homogenization requires repeated solutions of representative volume element (RVE) problems to determine the effective response transferred to the macroscale, including the homogenized stress. Here, we develop a basis-encoded, energy-based variational quantum formulation for linear-elastic computational homogenization. The reduced finite-element energy of the RVE is expressed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and mapped to an Ising Hamiltonian, whose ground state represents the equilibrium fluctuation field on the encoding grid. Using the Hellmann--Feynman theorem, differentiation of the strain-dependent Hamiltonian with respect to the prescribed macroscopic strain yields stress observables whose expectation values recover the conventional volume-averaged homogenized stress. A key structural result is that, although the equilibrium Hamiltonian is two-local, the stress observables are one-local. In linear elasticity, the two-qubit coupling coefficients are independent of macroscopic strain, hence, differentiation eliminates all two-qubit coupling terms, leaving only identity and single-qubit Pauli-$Z$ operators. These observables are diagonal in the same computational basis as the Ising Hamiltonian and can therefore be evaluated from the same computational-basis measurements used for the energy. Consequently, the homogenized stress can be obtained directly from the optimized quantum state using the same computational basis measurements as the Ising Hamiltonian, without requiring reconstruction of the microscopic displacement field or an additional measurement basis. Ground states are approximated using the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE), and the formulation is demonstrated on a two-dimensional periodic RVE.

Abstract: Under Pauli noise in a stabilizer memory, attributing a quantum error-correction failure to the noise that caused it has a canonical answer; under non-Pauli noise the attribution depends on how trajectories are paired with their counterfactuals. We define a failing shot's cause set, the sites whose removal alone removes the failure, and separate it from pairing-independent net effects. The pairing enters wherever a fixed random draw meets a Born probability; making it maximal at every random step selects shared random numbers, while continuity does not. In a distance-three surface code, exact non-Pauli noise and its Pauli twirl fail at rates equivalent within a registered margin, yet under shared draws 20.2% of exact-noise failures need three or more sites, against 2.3% under the twirl. Other admissible pairings move the mean coherent cause count from 1.61 to 80 times the minimum the marginals allow, yet pairing-independent quantities barely separate the two models. Abstract: We study the computational complexity of estimating observables and sampling from stationary states, or fixed points, of bosonic Lindbladian dynamics with bounded-degree polynomial interactions. While the complexity of transient bosonic dynamics has been investigated, understanding the computational power of bosonic fixed points requires addressing subtleties of infinite-dimensional systems, including the existence, uniqueness, and convergence of stationary states.

First, we show that universal quantum computation can be embedded into observable estimation at the unique fixed point of a bosonic Lindbladian with a Hamiltonian of degree at most twelve and only linear photon loss. Moreover, this fixed point can be prepared from vacuum in polynomial time. On the classical side, we identify Gaussian regimes with incoherent pumping that admit efficient photon-number sampling and establish stability of classical approximability under sufficiently weak non-Gaussian Kerr perturbations. Conversely, for Gaussian fixed points without incoherent pumping, we establish hardness of exact sampling under constant-rate linear loss and hardness of approximate sampling at inverse-polynomial loss rates under standard boson-sampling conjectures. We also relate sampling from non-Gaussian fixed points to variants of boson sampling. Finally, we prove that deciding the existence of a stationary density operator is $\mathsf{RE}$-hard, even for purely dissipative Lindbladians with degree-five jump operators acting on at most four modes.

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for sensing time-dependent fields through the lens of classical and quantum algorithms. First, we develop three pulse sequence protocols, namely randomized, matched, and pooled spectroscopy, which outperform established approaches such as dynamical decoupling across a wide range of tasks. These protocols attain the optimal sensitivity for linear and nonlinear AC sensing on conventional spin or qubit platforms, improving existing devices by changing only their pulse sequences. We then introduce quantum computational spectroscopy (QCS), which couples quantum sensors to a quantum processor. QCS combines two new primitives: a quantum computational frequency comb that coherently converts any classical signal into a quantum oracle, and a family of quantum algorithms that use the oracle to extract signal information in superposition. Variations can be implemented on current platforms with only one memory ancilla. We show that the ultimate limit imposed by quantum mechanics lies polynomially beyond the Heisenberg limit, and that QCS achieves it. Attaining this new limit requires quantum computation, establishing a fundamental separation between conventional and quantum computational sensing. Moreover, QCS retains beyond-Heisenberg scaling for broadband signals under constant-strength sensor depolarization, while we prove that the same noise limits any protocol lacking quantum computation to standard-quantum-limit scaling. Our result provides the first asymptotic, beyond-quadratic quantum advantage in AC metrology when noise is aligned with the signal, proving that Lindblad span conditions do not apply to broadband quantum sensing. Our work builds an algorithmic theory for designing sensing protocols and establishes quantum sensing as a new frontier for quantum algorithms, with quantum advantages that surpass the Heisenberg limit and that persist under noise.

Abstract: We present Gate-Error Amplification for Recalibration with Syndromes (GEARS), a runtime calibration framework that uses gate-error amplification to efficiently tune control parameters during quantum error correction. An elegant alternative uses syndrome information already collected during error correction as feedback for in situ recalibration, but acquiring signal requires sufficient (1) measurement budget or (2) probe strength, i.e., the size of deliberate control perturbations. More measurements improve statistical precision but result in slower control updates, while stronger perturbations produce larger calibration signals at the cost of additional errors. Our approach repeats selected CZ gates and probes their controls in spatially separated groups, strengthening local calibration signals while limiting the logical cost of probing. Surface code simulations show that GEARS with depth-three amplification reduces measurement requirements by approximately a factor of eight at matched gradient SNR and fixed probe strength. In closed-loop calibration, GEARS with depth-three amplification reduces excess expected logical failures by approximately 31-40% relative to calibration without amplification across the evaluated execution windows. These results indicate that deliberately amplifying gate errors during operation can improve the long-time performance of quantum computations.

Abstract: A central challenge in actuarial science is characterising the distribution of losses generated by an insurance portfolio. Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires high-fidelity entangling gates that can be implemented efficiently across multiple qubit pairs. Motivation: QEM 的通用下界表明，无问题特定信息地恢复无噪期望值可能需要超多项式甚至指数级采样开销；但二分类只需正确预测类别（依赖输出的符号与间隔），而非逐点精确重建每个无噪期望值，因此问题特定信息有望降低采样需求。

Method: 构建理论与实证框架，在相同学习目标下比较噪声参数化量子电路的 boosting 与概率误差消除（PEC）。针对指定电路结构与 Pauli 噪声，建立统一的假设类复杂度界：达到匹配的经验间隔损失即给出相同的期望分类风险上界；推导资源判据，并在有限采样 teacher-student 模拟中评估 AdaBoostR 与 Real AdaBoost，扫描电路深度与物理噪声强度。

Result: 在相同分类精度目标下，随深度与噪声增强，boosting 所需电路执行次数少于 PEC；且 boosting 的测试精度高于对应参考方法（PEC）。

Conclusion: 在所研究的监督学习问题中体现了任务依赖的资源优势：利用噪声输出中保留的信息可避免理想输出重建的开销，同时不牺牲观测到的分类性能。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation (QEM) can recover information about ideal quantum computations from noisy circuit executions, but may require a large sampling overhead. Fundamental lower bounds show that recovering noiseless observables without problem-specific information can require superpolynomially many samples, with exponential overhead for broad classes of noisy circuits. However, problem-specific information can reduce the number of samples needed to obtain the required answer. We investigate this possibility in supervised quantum machine learning, where labeled examples provide information about the function to be learned. Binary classification provides such a setting, since correct prediction depends on the sign and margin of the classifier output rather than on accurate reconstruction of every noiseless expectation value. We develop a theoretical and empirical framework for comparing boosting of noisy parameterized quantum circuits with probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), requiring both methods to reach the same learning target. For the specified circuit architectures under Pauli noise, we establish a common hypothesis-class complexity bound: matching empirical margin loss gives the same upper bound on expected classification risk. We derive resource criteria and evaluate AdaBoostR and Real AdaBoost in finite-shot teacher-student simulations across circuit depth and physical noise strength. Under the same classification-accuracy target, boosting requires fewer circuit executions as depth and noise increase. Boosting also achieves higher test accuracy than the corresponding reference. For the supervised-learning problems studied, these results demonstrate a task-dependent resource advantage: exploiting information retained in noisy outputs can avoid the cost of ideal-output reconstruction without sacrificing observed classification performance.

</details>

*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan,Mingsheng Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示量子差分隐私中的"纠缠攻击"：对手提交纠缠态的一半、保留另一半并联合测量，可使有限隐私参数趋于无穷。为此提出纠缠辅助量子差分隐私（EA-QDP）框架，给出有限EA-QDP参数的精确判据，证明最优参数可由SDP计算，并量化纠缠导致的额外隐私泄露，推荐使用去极化信道；在模拟与真实量子硬件上验证了攻击与防御。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子差分隐私框架只考虑对发布态的测量，忽略了提交态可能与对手秘密持有的参考系统纠缠，这种纠缠无法从提交态本身检测，构成经典情形中不存在的挑战。

Method: 提出纠缠攻击模型（对手提交纠缠态一半、保留另一半并联合测量）；进而定义纠缠辅助量子差分隐私（EA-QDP），刻画机制具有有限EA-QDP参数的充要条件，并将最优参数ε*_EA的求解归结为半定规划（SDP）；据此量化纠缠引起的额外隐私泄露，并分析可通过机制选择加以约束。

Result: 证明纠缠攻击可使满足有限量子差分隐私参数的机制隐私参数发散至无穷；给出EA-QDP最优参数的精确SDP刻画；证明选用合适机制（推荐去极化信道）可界定纠缠带来的额外隐私泄露；在模拟器与物理量子硬件上的实验与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 量子差分隐私必须考虑纠缠辅助的对手模型，EA-QDP为对抗纠缠攻击提供了原生且可计算（SDP）的隐私框架，去极化信道是控制额外泄露的推荐机制。

Abstract: Differential privacy has been successfully extended to quantum computing and information processing, where sensitive data are encoded in quantum states and protected by quantum noise mechanisms that keep the outcomes of any measurement on neighboring states nearly indistinguishable. In the quantum setting, however, the state submitted to a quantum computer may be entangled with a hidden reference system secretly held by an adversary. Such entanglement cannot be detected from the submitted state alone, and it poses a challenge with no counterpart in the classical setting. Existing frameworks of quantum differential privacy consider only measurements on the released state, leaving this scenario largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we identify the entanglement attack: an adversary submits one half of an entangled state to a quantum computer that runs a privacy mechanism, keeps the other half, and finally measures both halves jointly. We show that this attack may completely break a mechanism that satisfies quantum differential privacy with a finite parameter, driving its privacy parameter to infinity. To counter it, we propose entanglement-assisted quantum differential privacy (EA-QDP), a privacy framework that is native to quantum entanglement and remains valid against such attacks. We characterize exactly when a mechanism has a finite EA-QDP parameter, and show that the optimal parameter $\varepsilon^*_{\mathrm{EA}}$ of any quantum mechanism can be computed by a semidefinite programming problem (SDP). Building on this, we quantify the extra privacy leakage caused by entanglement and show that it can be bounded by choosing appropriate mechanisms, with the depolarizing channel being our recommended choice. Finally, we demonstrate both the attack and its countermeasure on simulated and physical quantum hardware, and the experimental results agree with our theoretical predictions.

</details>

Conclusion: 按连通性分组的共享门序合成在保证可扩展性的同时优于逐校验独立优化，在逻辑错误率、电路级距离与编译时间上均取得改进，可作为结构化CSS码门序优化的实用方案。

Abstract: Gate orders can change fault propagation and logical error rates even when syndrome-extraction circuits implement the same ideal stabilizer measurements. Optimizing these orderings independently for every check, however, becomes computationally demanding as code size increases. We present a synthesis method for structured Calderbank-Shor-Steane codes that groups checks by recurring connectivity and shares one relative gate order per group, reducing independent ordering choices. A CP-SAT solver jointly determines the shared orders and the execution time of each physical gate, enforcing resource and measurement constraints across the circuit. Noisy circuit simulations with decoding then guide the selection of the final circuit. We compare with state-of-the-art approaches, including Auto-Stabilizer-Check (ASC), PropHunt, and AlphaSyndrome. Across 15 quantum low-density parity-check codes from ten code families, our method achieves the lowest logical error rate estimates. Relative to ASC, the strongest baseline in our evaluation, the median reduction is 11.4% and the largest is 38.6%. Our circuits improve circuit-level distance over ASC on ten instances and preserve the code distance in seven. Across five timed codes, our method reduces the mean time to the first feasible circuit by factors of 21.4-136.6 relative to ASC, at equal or lower depth.

</details>

Abstract: Using time-dependent many-body simulations, we investigate nonmagnetic impurity effects on vortex-core Majorana braiding. Slowing the braid or using a $2a_0$ detour restores near-quantization in the cases examined. In the four-MZM configuration, attractive impurities leave the low-energy spectrum nearly unchanged, whereas repulsive impurities mainly suppress the first excited state. Slower braiding substantially improves the $X$ gate success probability. A specific attractive-impurity pair yields near-unit success ($\approx 0.9985$), unlike two weak repulsive impurities. These findings clarify how nonmagnetic impurities affect the low-energy spectrum and adiabatic dynamics of vortex-core Majorana braiding and provide theoretical guidance for designing robust braiding protocols in realistic topological superconducting platforms.

Abstract: Quantum computation entails robustness engineering: a noisy quantum state is measured continuously, and a classical algorithm (the decoder) must infer corrections that reliably remove the noise, fast enough to keep up. Written in the language, the published decoders show their parameters in plain view, allowing us to re-parameterise BP+OSD (ordered statistics decoding) and BP+LSD (localized statistics decoding) decoders with damping, before any search, making each 17.1--41.0$\times$ faster at a higher error rate, and BP+OSD-0 about 103 faster than the ldpc package's version tuned on the [[72,12,6]] bivariate-bicycle code. Frozen at one operating point, the two fastest evolved decoders generalize well across noise levels, code sizes and round counts, staying at the time-error front. Abstract: Long-lived qubits are attractive for storing quantum information, but they are often difficult to control or couple directly. A common strategy is to use mediator qubits to generate effective interactions for gate implementation. However, the mediator typically has a shorter coherence time than the data qubits, so mediator relaxation and dephasing are expected to limit the achievable gate fidelity. Here, we show that this limitation can be overcome. We present a reset-assisted control method that suppresses mediator-induced errors and enables high-fidelity single- and two-qubit gates on a quantum register of long-lived data qubits. Although mediator quantum jumps render the data-qubit evolution stochastic, repeated mediator reinitialization drives the resulting evolution paths toward the desired target operation. An analytical quantum-trajectory analysis reveals the physical mechanism of error suppression and provides compact fidelity formulas for adapting the method to different platforms. Our results relax the requirement for long mediator coherence, broadening the range of mediators that can support high-fidelity control.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知分布的熵估计是统计性质估计中的基本问题，而Rényi熵在固定整数阶 $k\geq 2$ 下的量子查询复杂性此前缺乏系统刻画，亟需在纯化量子查询访问模型下给出更优的量子算法框架。

Method: 在给定分布的多副本纯化量子查询访问下，构造"碰撞态"（collision states）的递归方案：保留并复用中间态，同时界定制备与反射代价，并为各阶选择保留态数目以使所有递归步骤共享同一查询预算；结合递减概率阈值序列，将沿路发现的标签贡献与剩余幂和分别估计；高精度区间采用批估计器（batch estimator），并用初始搜索消除对幂和的先验知识依赖。

Result: 算法以 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(n^{u_k}/\varepsilon^{1-u_k} + n^{(k-1)/(2k)}\varepsilon\right)$ 次查询实现 $k$ 阶Rényi熵的加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 估计，其中 $u_k=\max_{2\leq j\leq k}\frac{(3k-j)2^{j-2}-k}{k(2^j-1)}$。

Conclusion: 提出了一套用于固定整数阶Rényi熵估计的量子算法框架，其核心是可复用中间态的碰撞态递归构造与阈值分解估计策略，在查询复杂度上给出了显式指数刻画。

Abstract: Entropy estimation for unknown distributions is a basic problem in statistical property estimation. In this paper, we propose a quantum algorithm framework for estimating Rényi entropy with fixed integer order $k\geq 2$. Given purified quantum query-access to a distribution on $n$ symbols, our algorithm estimates its order-$k$ Rényi entropy to within additive error $ε$ using $$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{n^{u_k}}{ε^{1-u_k}} + \frac{n^{(k-1)/(2k)}}ε\right)$$ queries, where $u_k=\max_{2\leq j\leq k} \frac{(3k-j)2^{j-2}-k}{k(2^j-1)}$. The main ingredient is a recursive construction of collision states in which intermediate states are retained and reused. We bound both the preparation and reflection costs, and choose the number of retained states at each order so that all recursive steps fit within a common query budget. We combine this construction with a sequence of decreasing probability thresholds, estimating the contribution of the labels found along the way separately from the remaining power sum. A batch estimator gives the required bound in the high-precision regime, and an initial search removes the need for prior knowledge of the power sum.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带中的紧凑局域态（CLS）与连续谱束缚态（BIC）都是空间局域现象，但二者通常源于不同机制；作者希望厘清二者在非厄米体系中的内在联系。

Abstract: Quantum communication provides a natural means to transmit quantum information, but how difficult is it to make such a transmission using only classical communication resources? It reveals the rapidly scaling fundamental cost of classical simulation. Moreover, we develop a toolbox of methods to certify classical complexity in quantum preparation devices, including an exact semidefinite-programming characterisation, analytical witnesses for symmetric preparations and convex programming scalable up to hundreds and sometimes thousands of qubit states. ### [67] [Ellis: Extremely Fast Quantum Circuit Simulation under Qubit Loss and Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26173)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特丢失(qubit loss)与泄漏(leakage)是持续型错误：一旦某比特处于丢失或泄漏状态，作用其上的所有操作都被丢弃，导致实际执行的 Clifford 电路逐 shot 变化。这破坏了 Pauli-frame 模拟的前提——后者需将所有采样得到的 Pauli 翻转 XOR 到单一"公共参考"测量记录上以构造每个 shot。

Method: 1) 重新审视 tableau 模拟与 Pauli-frame 模拟的分工；2) 表明在丢失模型下仍有若干关键容错原语保持公共参考，包括 CSS 码存储、格点手术(lattice surgery)与横向 CNOT；3) 定义 frame-CSS 电路：在给定"比特可在何处丢失与重载"的丢失模型下，至多相差局域单比特基变换即为 CSS 的电路；4) 用带逐 shot 掩码(dropped 操作掩码)的 Pauli frame 进行精确模拟；5) 证明所有 frame-CSS 电路均具公共参考，并给出近线性时间的认证算法；6) 在开源包 Ellis 中实现认证器与高性能模拟器。

Result: 在 gross code 上同时存在比特丢失与 Pauli 错误的场景下，Ellis 单 CPU 核可达 952,000 shots/s，比 Clifft 与 PPVM 至少快 1000 倍。

Conclusion: frame-CSS 这一电路类为持续型丢失/泄漏误差提供了带公共参考的精确 Pauli-frame 模拟路径，兼顾理论可证性与工程效率，可作为容错量子计算大规模采样的实用工具。

Abstract: Qubit loss and leakage are persistent errors: while a qubit remains lost or leaked, every operation acting on it is dropped. Consequently, the Clifford circuit executed differs from shot to shot. This variation breaks Pauli-frame simulation, which constructs each shot by XORing sampled Pauli flips onto a single \emph{common reference} measurement record. By rethinking the roles of tableau simulation and Pauli-frame simulation, we show that many key fault-tolerant primitives nevertheless retain a common reference under loss. These include CSS code memory, lattice surgery, and transversal CNOT. Such circuits can therefore be simulated exactly under Pauli noise and loss by a Pauli frame with per-shot masks for the dropped operations. We define \emph{frame-CSS} circuits as those that are CSS up to local single-qubit basis changes, given a loss model that specifies where qubits can be lost and reloaded. We prove that every frame-CSS circuit has a common reference, and we give a practically near-linear-time procedure that certifies whether a circuit is frame-CSS. We implement the certifier and a high-performance simulator in the open-source package \href{https://github.com/isaachyw/ELLIS}{\textsc{Ellis}} (Efficient Loss and Leakage Invariant-reference Simulation). On the gross code under qubit loss and Pauli errors, \ellis{} samples 952{,}000 shots per second on a single CPU core, at least $1000\times$ faster than Clifft~\cite{chase2026} and PPVM~\cite{luo2026ppvm}.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In quantum communications and other photon-counting applications, reliably detecting weak optical signals against a strong noise background remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we develop a simple idea: when a signal is carried by pairs (or more generally multiplets) of photons emitted simultaneously, postselection of coincident detections recorded on two detectors makes it possible to improve-sometimes drastically-the Signal to Noise Ratio. Essentially, this is made possible by the fact that genuine and accidental coincidences scale differently with the width of the postselection window. As long as the window remains wide enough to retain detections from the same pair, the genuine coincidence rate is approximately unchanged. In contrast, coincidences formed from independent clicks decrease linearly as the window is narrowed. The resulting signal-to-noise ratio, therefore, increases inversely with the window width. Our approach makes it possible to reveal photon-pair signals that are otherwise hidden beneath a much stronger uncorrelated background. We analyze here applications of this idea to quantum key distribution, metrology, and anti-counterfeiting.

</details>

Abstract: Single-photon emitters are one of the key elements in state-of-the art quantum technologies, yet optimizing and manipulating their emission requires precise fabrication of the emitter systems themselves. Method: 利用 QR 分解在重叠块间转移酉因子，保持整体变换不变同时重塑局部合成目标；对终点前允许测量的块，构建无辅助比特的动态实现，采用带 CNOT 预算的测量前搜索与解析分支酉构造，最小化期望 CNOT 成本。

Result: 随机电路目标上相对 QFAST、SQUANDER、Fast-USYN 平均双比特门数分别降低 28.79%–42.52%、2.05%–52.62%、30.51%–74.64%；4–6 比特量子算法基准上分别降低 27.27%–36.77%、20.65%–69.10%、36.31%–71.88%。

Conclusion: 跨块酉重分配与基于测量的动态重构可有效降低合成电路的期望双比特门开销。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuit synthesis jointly searches circuit structure and continuous parameters to reduce costly two-qubit gates, but block-based workflows typically instantiate blocks independently, limiting simplification across block boundaries. Moreover, fully unitary synthesis does not exploit opportunities created by permitted output measurements and classical feedforward. We present CAVQS, a context-aware variational synthesis framework that combines unitary redistribution and dynamic reconstruction. First, CAVQS uses QR decomposition to transfer unitary factors between overlapping blocks, preserving the overall transformation while reshaping local synthesis targets to enable cross-block reductions. Second, for blocks that precede permitted terminal measurements, CAVQS constructs ancilla-free dynamic implementations with a CNOT-budgeted pre-measurement search and analytic branch-unitary construction, thereby minimizing expected CNOT cost. Across random-circuit targets, CAVQS reduces mean two-qubit gate counts by 28.79%-42.52%, 2.05%-52.62%, and 30.51%-74.64% relative to QFAST, SQUANDER, and Fast-USYN, respectively, on common successful instances. On 4--6-qubit quantum algorithm benchmarks, the corresponding reductions are 27.27%-36.77%, 20.65%-69.10%, and 36.31%-71.88%, respectively.

</details>


### [72] [A sensitivity-constrained polynomial method for DQC1 query complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26231)
*Changpeng Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为DQC1查询模型建立了多项式方法的类比物：引入Boolean函数的"敏感性近似度"（sensitivity approximate degree）作为DQC1查询复杂度的下界，证明任何非常数全函数的DQC1查询复杂度为Ω(N)，并刻画出对称部分布尔函数的该测度，给出DQC1与BQP查询复杂度之间的紧二次分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式方法是证明量子查询复杂度下界的核心工具，但此前主要适用于BQP查询模型。DQC1（单比特纯态、其余为最大混合态）作为受限量子模型，缺乏类似的代数下界工具，因此需要发展对应的多项式方法框架，以刻画DQC1模型的计算能力边界。

Method: 通过构造DQC1查询模型中接受概率的多项式表示，定义Boolean函数的"敏感性近似度"这一新测度，并证明其是DQC1查询复杂度的下界；随后建立该测度的若干结构性质，对对称部分布尔函数给出基于正反标记Hamming权重层间隔的完整刻画，并推导DQC1算法的定量平均情形经典模拟定理，最后应用于Fourier分析框架。

Result: (1) 任意N变量的非常数全布尔函数敏感性近似度为Ω(N)，故DQC1需Ω(N)次查询；(2) 对称部分布尔函数上敏感性近似度与标准近似度存在紧二次分离；(3) 由此得到DQC1与BQP查询复杂度间的紧二次分离；(4) 给出DQC1算法的定量平均情形经典模拟定理；(5) 获得2-Forrelation问题Ω(N^{1/4})的DQC1查询下界的简洁证明。

Conclusion: DQC1查询模型与BQP存在本质差异：对全布尔函数无渐近量子加速，但部分函数上至多二次分离；敏感性近似度作为DQC1版多项式方法，为受限量子模型的查询下界提供了系统性工具，并可与平均情形模拟和Fourier分析技术结合。

Abstract: The polynomial method is one of the fundamental techniques for proving lower bounds on quantum query complexity, particularly in the BQP query model. In this work, we develop an analogue of the polynomial method for the DQC1 query model. We introduce the notion of {\em sensitivity approximate degree} for Boolean functions and show that it provides a lower bound on DQC1 query complexity. We establish several basic structural properties of this new measure. As a first result, we show that every nonconstant total Boolean function on $N$ input variables has sensitivity approximate degree $Ω(N)$, and hence requires $Ω(N)$ queries in the DQC1 model. Thus, unlike in the BQP query model, DQC1 admits no asymptotic query speedup for total Boolean functions. For symmetric partial Boolean functions, we give a complete characterization of the sensitivity approximate degree in terms of the separation between oppositely labeled Hamming weight layers. This yields a tight quadratic separation between sensitivity approximate degree and standard approximate degree, and also leads to tight quadratic separations between DQC1 and BQP query complexities. We further establish quantitative average-case classical simulation theorems for DQC1 algorithms. Finally, we apply our framework to Fourier analysis, obtaining a simple proof of an $Ω(N^{1/4})$ DQC1 query lower bound for the 2-Forrelation problem.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Hamiltonian Identification via Quantum Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26241)
*Jawaher Kaldari,Saif Al-Kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将哈密顿量辨识建模为量子强化学习（QRL）问题：Vanilla-QRL处理稳态动力学，GRU-QRL跟踪非稳态动力学，在横向场Ising模型上与QGRNN基线对比，GRU-QRL在非稳态下跟踪显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子系统的支配动力学可能因参数漂移、控制条件变化或工作区间切换而随时间变化，而精确的哈密顿量辨识对系统表征、器件校准、仿真与硬件可靠运行至关重要，因此需要具备自适应跟踪能力的辨识工具。

Method: 把哈密顿量辨识形式化为量子强化学习问题：量子智能体处理序列化的量子态观测并估计哈密顿量参数。先用Vanilla-QRL处理稳态动力学，再引入带GRU的循环QRL架构以跟踪非稳态动力学。在横向场Ising模型上设置五类场景评估：稳态、哈密顿量突变切换、快速变化、长轨迹随机切换位置、渐进漂移；并与Quantum Graph Recurrent Neural Network（QGRNN）基线对比，同时检验冻结策略在未见轨迹上的表现。

Result: 两种方法均能准确重构稳态哈密顿量；在非稳态动力学下GRU-QRL的跟踪性能显著优于QGRNN。

Conclusion: 循环QRL具备作为自适应系统辨识工具的潜力，可用于监测时变量子系统。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the Hamiltonian governing a quantum system is important for quantum-system characterization, device calibration, simulation, and reliable operation of quantum hardware. In practical systems, however, the governing dynamics may vary over time because of parameter drift, changing control conditions, or transitions between operating regimes. In this work, we formulate Hamiltonian identification as a quantum reinforcement learning (QRL) problem in which a quantum agent processes sequential quantum-state observations and estimates the governing Hamiltonian parameters. We first consider stationary dynamics using a Vanilla-QRL agent and then introduce a recurrent GRU-QRL architecture to track non-stationary dynamics. The framework is evaluated on transverse-field Ising model systems under stationary dynamics, abrupt Hamiltonian switching, rapidly changing Hamiltonians, random switch locations over longer trajectories, and gradual Hamiltonian drift. We further compare the proposed approach with a Quantum Graph Recurrent Neural Network (QGRNN) baseline and evaluate frozen policies on unseen trajectories. Results show that both approaches accurately reconstruct stationary Hamiltonians, while GRU-QRL provides substantially improved tracking under non-stationary dynamics. These results demonstrate the potential of recurrent QRL as an adaptive system-identification tool for monitoring time-varying quantum systems.

</details>


### [74] [Efficient calculation of the full Laguerre-Gaussian spectrum of entangled biphotons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26251)
*Jiabao Peng,Richard Bernecker,Stephan Fritzsche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出生成函数方法，无需近似相位匹配函数即可解析完成SPDC空间双光子态的四维横向空间积分，得到可高效、稳定计算任意径向与OAM指标的完整LG谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPDC空间双光子态常用LG模描述，但计算任意径向与轨道角动量指标的完整LG谱需高维数值积分，计算量大且高阶模数值不稳定，成为高维空间纠缠表征与调控的障碍。

Method: 引入生成函数方法，对四维横向空间积分作解析求解且不近似相位匹配函数；用少数无量纲参数完整刻画源几何，对波矢简并且腰斑相等的光子可约化为两个参数；据此导出完整LG谱的解析表达式。

Result: 所得表达式在高阶模下可高效、数值稳定地求值；作为示例，比较了投影到最低径向阶与径向不敏感探测两种方式得到的OAM谱，并研究了通过调节源参数逼近高维最大纠缠的途径。

Conclusion: 该工作为高效表征与调控SPDC中的高维空间纠缠提供了新的解析工具。

Abstract: The spatial biphoton state generated by spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) enables applications in high-dimensional quantum information processing and is often described in Laguerre-Gaussian (LG) modes. However, calculating the full LG spectrum for arbitrary radial and orbital angular momentum (OAM) indices requires computationally demanding multidimensional integrations. We present a generating-function approach that analytically performs the four-dimensional transverse spatial integral without approximating the phase-matching function. The source geometry can be completely described by a few dimensionless parameters, which reduce to two parameters for wave-vector-degenerate photons with equal waists. Our derived expressions for the full LG spectrum allow for computationally efficient and numerically stable evaluation at high mode orders, addressing a crucial obstacle in previous works. To illustrate our approach, we compare OAM spectra obtained by projection onto the lowest radial order and by radially insensitive detection, and investigate how high-dimensional maximal entanglement can be approached by tuning source parameters. Our results provide a new analytical tool for efficiently characterizing and engineering high-dimensional spatial entanglement in SPDC.

</details>


### [75] [A Tensor-Algebraic No-Go Theorem for High-Dimensional Photonic Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger States Discovered with AlphaProof Nexus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26300)
*Mario Krenn,Moritz Firsching,George Tsoukalas,Rishikesh Gajjala,Xuemei Gu,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：对任意偶数 N≥4，当维度 D≥N 时，不存在复权重单色量子图，即概率性对源无法生成 N 光子 D 维 GHZ 态；证明由 AI 辅助的 Lean 形式化搜索（AlphaProof Nexus）发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子 GHZ 态可用边着色复权重图表示，生成 N 光子 D 维 GHZ 态等价于构造单色量子图。需要判定这种构造在何种参数下不可能，从而界定概率性对源方案的适用边界。

Method: 将输出振幅写成完美匹配的相干求和；构造振幅的加权和 𝓕，其系数按顶点逐个分解；对某一固定顶点的颜色求和，把问题约化为 (N−1)×N 的有效边权矩阵；利用行—列不平衡性得到 𝓕(ω)=0 而 𝓕(Δ)≠0。证明在 Lean 中经 AlphaProof Nexus 辅助形式化搜索获得。

Result: 对任意偶数 N≥4 且 D≥N，不存在复权重单色量子图；由 𝓕(ω)=0 与 𝓕(Δ)≠0 与所需等式 ω=Δ 矛盾即得。此外给出该数学证明的物理解释。

Conclusion: D≥N、N≥4 偶数的情形下，基于概率性对源的光子 GHZ 态生成在单色量子图表述下不可行；该不可能性结论由 AI 发现并形式化验证的证明确立。

Abstract: Photonic GHZ states generated with probabilistic pair sources can be described by edge-coloured, complex-weighted graphs. In this representation, conditioning on an event with one photon in every detector turns the output amplitudes into coherent sums over perfect matchings, and generating an \(N\)-photon \(D\)-dimensional GHZ state is equivalent to constructing a monochromatic quantum graph. Here, we prove that no complex-weighted monochromatic quantum graph exists for any even \(N\ge4\) when \(D\ge N\). The proof, discovered through AI-assisted formal proof search in Lean using AlphaProof Nexus, constructs a weighted sum \(\mathcal F\) of amplitudes whose coefficients factor vertex by vertex. Summing over the colours at one fixed vertex reduces the problem to an \((N-1)\times N\) matrix of effective edge weights. For the graph amplitudes \(ω\) and GHZ target \(Δ\), the row--column imbalance yields \(\mathcal F(ω)=0\) but \(\mathcal F(Δ)\neq0\), contradicting the required equality \(ω=Δ\). Finally, we give a physical interpretation of the AI-discovered mathematical proof.

</details>


### [76] [All Non-Contextual Pauli Hamiltonians are Free-Fermion in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26315)
*Andrew M. Projansky,Weishi Wang,Kevin A. Reiss,James D. Whitfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究非语境Pauli哈密顿量与费米子物理的联系，证明这类哈密顿量是"自由费米子伪装"的且具有类Jordan–Wigner编码，并用Lean形式化；进而提出基于线图的启发式算法寻找分子哈密顿量的自由费米子片段，与搜索非语境子空间的基线在识别可解分量上性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将哈密顿量分解为经典可处理片段是量子模拟的核心任务，语境子空间分解（contextual subspace decomposition）是其中一种方法。由于语境性条件依赖于Pauli算符的对易与反对易关系，作者希望建立非语境性与费米子物理之间的显式联系，以明确哪些片段对量子模拟真正有用。

Method: 理论上提出两条定理：(1) 非语境Pauli哈密顿量是"自由费米子伪装"的（free-fermion-disguised）；(2) 将这类哈密顿量用Majorana与Dirac费米子算符表示，说明其携带类Jordan–Wigner的费米子-量子比特编码。两定理均在Lean证明助手中形式化，用以刻画费米子非语境哈密顿量空间及其与更广义自由费米子伪装哈密顿量类的关系。算法上提出一种基于线图（line graph）的启发式算法，用于寻找自由费米子片段及其连通分量，并在编码分子哈密顿量上与非语境搜索进行基准对比。

Result: 基于线图的启发式算法在识别自由费米子可解分量方面，与针对非语境子空间的搜索方法性能相当（comparable performance）。Lean形式化给出了非语境Pauli哈密顿量与费米子非语境哈密顿量空间的结构性刻画及其与自由费米子伪装类的包含/关联关系。

Conclusion: 非语境Pauli哈密顿量与自由费米子物理存在本质对应，可视为带类Jordan–Wigner编码的自由费米子伪装系统；线图启发式方法可作为寻找自由费米子片段的有效手段，其结果对判断量子模拟中何种哈密顿量片段具有实用价值具有指导意义。

Abstract: Decomposing Hamiltonians into classically tractable fragments is central to quantum simulation. One such way of doing so is the contextual subspace decomposition, which expresses the Hamiltonian with respect to non-contextual sets of Paulis. The conditions for contextuality rely on the anticommutation and commutation of Pauli operators; it is therefore natural to make an explicit connection between non-contextuality and fermionic physics. Theoretical work is centered around two theorems: in the first, we consider non-contextual Pauli Hamiltonians and show that they are free-fermion-disguised. In the second we write these Pauli non-contextual Hamiltonians in terms of Majorana and Dirac fermionic operators, showing that they carry a Jordan--Wigner-like fermion-to-qubit encoding. These two central theorems are formalized in the proof assistant language Lean. We use this to describe the space of fermionic non-contextual Hamiltonians, and highlight their relation to the more general class of free-fermion-disguised Hamiltonians. We further develop a heuristic algorithm to find line-graph based free-fermion fragments and their connected components of molecular Hamiltonians. We benchmark our algorithms against non-contextual searches for encoded molecular Hamiltonians and find comparable performance in identifying free-fermion solvable components. Our work has implications for what fragments to consider as useful for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [77] [Stitching Quantum Codes into Quilts: Universal Logic with no Ancillary Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26329)
*Stasiu Wolanski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"code quilt"构造：无需辅助系统，直接在两个或多个码块间测量目标联合逻辑Pauli乘积，仅用码块空闲综合提取已有的ancilla。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越表面码的容错逻辑Pauli乘积测量方案虽多，但通常依赖辅助系统或额外物理比特，需要更节省资源且可推广的构造。

Method: 基于子系统码给出quilt构造的结构分析与码容量距离保持的充分条件；提出两个协议变体（直接测量gauge生成元、物理比特最多增加2的实用电路；以及渐近性质更好但未一般性证明容错的变体）；对bivariate bicycle码与表面码做大规模经验分析，优化稀疏、低拥塞的detector基，并在部分电路上做解码实验。

Result: 为25,000个随机选取的逻辑算符找到保持码容量距离的quilt；为随机子集估计出保距电路；在144比特bivariate bicycle码与d=12旋转表面码上，物理错误率1.5×10⁻³至3×10⁻³时逻辑错误概率与空闲存储相当。

Conclusion: code quilt提供了一种无需ancilla系统的码间逻辑测量方案，第一变体实用但大距离极限下不可证容错，第二变体渐近更优，经验结果支持其性能可与空闲存储相比。

Abstract: Proposals abound for performing fault-tolerant logical Pauli-product measurements between quantum codes beyond the surface code. We introduce a new construction, the code quilt, whereby a target joint logical operator of two or more codeblocks is directly measured without the use of an ancillary system. We construct explicit circuits to perform a logical measurement using the quilt construction, which also require no physical qubits beyond the ancillas already in place to perform idle syndrome extraction on the codeblocks in question. We give a detailed structural analysis of the construction, which is based on a subsystem code, and provide several sets of sufficient conditions under which the relevant code-capacity distance is preserved. We give two variants of the protocol. The first, where the gauge generators of the subsystem code are measured directly, is provably not fault tolerant in the large-distance limit but admits a particularly practical circuit implementation in which the physical qubit degree increases by at most two. The second has more favourable asymptotic properties, although we do not prove its fault tolerance in the general case. We then perform an extensive empirical analysis of the technique, in particular of the first variant, as applied to bivariate bicycle and surface codes, finding code-capacity-distance preserving quilts for measuring 25,000 randomly selected logical operators. We optimise to find sparse and decongested detector bases for each and, for a randomly selected subset, find circuits that we estimate to be distance preserving. Finally, we perform decoding experiments on a subset of these circuits and find that their logical error probability is comparable to that of idle memory for operations involving the 144-qubit bivariate bicycle code and distance-12 rotated surface code at physical error rates between $1.5\times 10^{-3}$ and $3\times 10^{-3}$.

</details>


### [78] [Quantifying the irrealism required by quantum random access codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26340)
*Pedro R. Dieguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出量子随机存取码中"非实在性"（irrealism）与通信成功率之间的精确权衡（frontier）：在计算基/互无偏基解码下的PM码，各维数下精确求出最小总非实在性；将解码器也纳入优化时，超过经典值才需要正的非实在性；对EA码则把前沿增量刻画为实在论型非局域性，并给出基于成功率（>0.815）的共享态最小非局域性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：解码观测量必须偏离"具有实在性元素"多远，才能在通信任务中获得给定的量子优势。作者希望在量子随机存取码（QRAC）框架下定量化"非实在性"这一资源，从而把观测量层面的实在论违反与可操作的任务成功率联系起来。

Method: 定义观测量具有实在性元素为"未揭示测量不扰动制备态"，并以到最近的此类制备态的量子相对熵作为非实在性度量；由于该度量同时包含局部相干性与被未揭示测量破坏的关联，可统一适用于PM码与纠缠辅助（EA）码。对互无偏基解码的PM码，在各维数下精确求解"非实在性前沿"（给定成功概率下最小总非实在性），并给出过量非实在性恒等式，证明解码算符的Gibbs态唯一达到前沿。进一步对解码器联合优化；对EA码在qubit接收方、可信解码器、宇称相关发送设置的条件下，将前沿增量刻画为语境实在论型非局域性。

Result: 互无偏基解码的PM码：精确得到所有维数下总非实在性—成功率的权衡前沿，且Gibbs态唯一取到；联合优化解码器时，所需非实在性在经典值以内为零、超过经典值为正，并发现qubit情形下成功率低于0.833时互无偏解码器并非最省；EA码情形下前沿比PM情形高出的量等于最优码所用语境的实在论型非局域性，且当发送方为秩一投影测量时，成功率>0.815可认证共享态至少具有该值的实在论型非局域性。

Conclusion: 非实在性是刻画QRAC量子优势的合适且可操作资源量：它给出精确的资源前沿、唯一最优态（Gibbs态）与从观测成功率反推态非局域性的认证判据（0.815阈值），并为解码器选择提供反直觉结论（互无偏基在低成功率区并非最优）。

Abstract: How far must decoding observables be from having elements of reality to achieve a given quantum advantage in a communication task? We answer this question for quantum random access codes. Here, an observable has an element of reality when its unrevealed measurement leaves the preparation undisturbed; its irrealism is the relative entropy to the closest such preparation. Because it comprises local coherence and the correlations destroyed by the unrevealed measurement, irrealism applies to prepare-and-measure (PM) and entanglement-assisted (EA) codes alike. For PM codes decoded in mutually unbiased bases, we obtain the irrealism frontier, the least total irrealism compatible with each success probability, exactly in every dimension; an excess-irrealism identity shows that Gibbs states of the decoding operator attain it uniquely. For PM codes minimized also over the decoders, the required irrealism vanishes up to the classical value and is positive beyond it; for qubits, mutually unbiased decoders are not the cheapest below a success probability of 0.833. For EA codes with a qubit receiver, trusted decoders, and parity-dependent sender settings, the frontier exceeds the PM one by the realism-based nonlocality of the contexts used by an optimal code, and with rank-one projective sender measurements a success probability above 0.815 certifies a minimum realism-based nonlocality of the shared state.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate entanglement, readable records and quantum recovery using an exactly solvable chain of sequentially interacting qubits. For weak interactions, entanglement reaches a later environment site before that site carries an equally strong local record. The leading local record is encoded in an off-diagonal element of the reduced density matrix and can be detected by a Pauli measurement, while a population measurement gives a higher-order signal. We further characterize the accessibility of the relative phase through joint transverse correlations and derive the optimal fidelity for recovering an unknown quantum state from the system and an accessible environment fragment. We show that interactions acting only within the environment can redistribute readable records and change the region required for quantum recovery without affecting the reduced state of the system.

</details>
