Abstract: Motivated by the Page-Wootters construction in which time is a quantum clock reading and the global system state is stationary, we propose a quantum circuit that explores the observable consequences of treating measurement timing as a quantum degree of freedom. We place two Zeno measurement schedules of a driven qubit in a coherent superposition using a schedule qubit. We show that, for a fixed drive strength, the interference visibility between the two schedules depends only on the time offset between them, not on the number of intermediate measurements taken. Then we show that, for one schedule-readout outcome, the Zeno trend reverses, predicting a result that complements the standard Zeno probability. We propose a circuit implementation with physical memory qubits and show that measuring the memory register's joint $X$-basis parity distinguishes a coherent schedule from a classical or dephased one. We give the gate count and an error budget for a superconducting-qubit implementation. The Page-Wootters picture motivates this protocol and provides an equivalent stationary representation, although the predictions follow from the standard circuit description without requiring a relational-time interpretation. This is consistent with the Page-Wootters framework recovering standard quantum dynamics through correlations between a clock and the rest of the system.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics and the spontaneous decay of two identical two-level atoms into the vacuum in a cavity at low temperature. We analytically derive the survival probability and the amplitudes of the state of the system by calculating the exact self-energy when the atoms are both initially excited. It is found that moderate asymmetry enhances the decay of the survival probability and increases the entanglement production in either lossless and lossy cavities.

TL;DR: 本文给出一种高效经典随机算法，用于近似具有共轭跃迁矩阵与共轭配对轨道相互作用的费米子哈密顿量（含吸引型Hubbard模型）的基态能量，且在无需谱隙或弱耦合假设下成立。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 先将基态能量估计归约为配分函数估计；再用可控能量误差的离散化把问题转化为行列式模平方的非负加权和；随后应用Chen–Liu对数凹计数定理；并通过控制独立采样次数与矩阵更新次数、利用稀疏矩阵降低单次更新代价，得到更紧的算术复杂度界。

Result: 在不依赖物理谱隙或弱耦合假设的条件下，获得了多项式时间的高效经典随机近似保证，且适用范围超出在位密度吸引相互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为含符号问题障碍的费米子基态能量估计提供了严格的经典随机算法保证，拓展了可高效经典求解的相互作用费米子模型范围。

Abstract: Estimating the ground energy of interacting fermions is central to quantum many-body physics, and recent quantum algorithms provide polynomial-time guarantees for some structured families. Classical approaches commonly rely on quantum Monte Carlo, where the sign problem is a major obstacle. However, even when a nonnegative expansion without sign problem is available, rigorous guarantees for rapid mixing are still far from well understood. In this paper, we give an efficient classical randomized algorithm for approximating the ground energy of fermionic Hamiltonians with conjugate hopping matrices and conjugate-paired orbital interactions, which includes the attractive Hubbard models. This guarantee not only holds without a physical spectral gap or a weak-coupling assumption, but also covers interactions beyond onsite density attractions. Technically, we first reduce ground-energy estimation to partition-function estimation and then use a discretization with controlled energy error to obtain a nonnegative weighted sum of squared determinant moduli and then apply the Chen--Liu log-concave counting theorem. In addition, we obtain a sharper arithmetic bound by controlling how many independent sampling runs and matrix updates are needed, and using sparse matrices to reduce the cost of each update.

</details>


### [83] [Non-Binary Detector Error Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26395)
*Stefano Tinelli,Francisco Lázaro,Francisco García Herrero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出非二元探测器错误模型（non-binary DEM），将综合征提取电路每个故障位置建模为原始字母表上的单一随机变量，并给出split-merge-regroup变换以构建紧凑模型；在退化量子MAP解码下二元与非二元DEM等价，但量子MAP下不等价；数值上Tanner图边数减少4–5倍，BP及BP+OSD性能更优、CPU上快至9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子纠错中综合征提取电路本身有故障，Stim等现有工作普遍使用二元DEM，将CNOT等非二元电路错误拆解为独立二元错误机制，这种表示仅对可分解为独立二元部分的信道精确，存在表示误差与图冗余。

Method: 引入非二元DEM：每个故障位置用一个定义在原始字母表（如CNOT后的16个双比特Pauli错误）上的随机变量表示；提出split-merge-regroup变换，通过合并翻转相同探测器与观测量组合的错误分量，从任意Clifford电路构建紧凑非二元DEM；推导二元与非二元DEM在退化量子MAP解码下的等价性，并说明BP仍每边仅传单标量消息。

Result: 在surface code、color code和bivariate bicycle code上验证：非二元DEM的Tanner图边数减少4–5倍，利于硬件实现；在有无OSD后处理时BP性能均优于二元对应方案，BP+OSD在CPU上最高快9.4倍。

Conclusion: 非二元DEM可精确表示任意Pauli信道，在保持BP单标量消息形式的前提下同时获得更稀疏的因子图与更优的解码性能，是可替代二元DEM的紧凑高效建模方案。

Abstract: A key challenge in fault-tolerant quantum error correction (QEC) is that the syndrome extraction circuits are themselves faulty. Such circuit-level noise is commonly described by a detector error model (DEM). In Stim and in almost all related works, DEMs are binary: every non-binary circuit error (e.g., CNOT gate), is decomposed into independent binary error mechanisms. In this work, we introduce non-binary DEMs, in which each fault location of the syndrome extraction circuit is modeled by one random variable over its original alphabet, e.g., the 16 two-qubit Pauli errors after a CNOT gate. We also propose a split-merge-regroup transformation that builds a compact non-binary DEM from any Clifford circuit by merging the error components that flip the same detectors and observables. Non-binary DEMs represent any Pauli channel exactly, whereas binary DEMs are exact only for channels that factorize into independent binary parts. When the channels factorize, we prove that binary and non-binary DEMs are equivalent under degenerate quantum maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) decoding, but not under quantum MAP decoding. Although the variables are non-binary, belief propagation (BP) still exchanges a single scalar message per edge. Our numerical results with surface, color and bivariate bicycle codes show that non-binary DEMs have two main advantages. First, their Tanner graphs have four to five times fewer edges, which is advantageous for hardware implementations. Second, their performance under BP, with and without OSD post-processing, is better than that of their binary counterparts, and BP with OSD runs up to $9.4\times$ faster in a CPU.

</details>


### [84] [When lies look like noise: certifying untrusted entanglement swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26436)
*Maria Gragera Garces,Chris Heunen,Mahesh K. Marina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声网络中纠缠交换的安全性：中间节点忠实执行量子操作但可能谎报经典结果，证明理想纠缠交换功能无法实现，进而提出一种割择（cut-and-choose）协议，以随机抽取子集测试来认证其余配对，并在有限尺度和内存约束下给出服务质量保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠交换是量子中继器实现远距离纠缠分发的关键，是量子互联网的前提。若中间节点恶意谎报看似无碍的经典测量结果，终端的纠缠保真度与安全性便无从保证；而现有基于验证的方案会消耗被认证的量子态，缺乏可用的安全性界定。

Method: 在半诚实但可能谎报的网络威胁模型下做不可实现性分析：证明理想纠缠交换功能赋予对手对交付态无任何影响，而重标记报告的节点却有影响，故理想功能不可实现；加入诚实噪声亦无济于事，除非理想设定同时让出有界比例的误报配对。据此提出割择协议：随机测试一部分配对来认证剩余配对，由于测试无法区分误报与读出噪声，协议认证的是高于预先约定的最小噪声水平的误差，即威胁模型下的误报率。

Result: 在10^6个配对上认证1%误报率需10^4至1.7×10^5个测试配对（对应最小噪声水平0–5%）；主要开销是内存，1%的认证需同时持有10^5–10^6个配对，远超现有量子存储能力。采用分块验证与释放可将内存绑定到块大小，代价是速率下降：10^4量子比特的存储可在4.7×10^5个交付配对上认证14%。

Conclusion: 纠缠交换在"诚实量子操作+可谎报经典结果"的威胁模型下不存在可实现的理想功能，须以显式噪声让步换取安全性。割择协议提供可量化的服务质量保证：失败即证明节点未按约定交付；且未测试配对仍可用于后续使用，并具有限尺度保证。实际瓶颈为内存，分块方案可在现有存储规模下权衡速率与可认证的误报率。

Abstract: Entanglement swapping lets quantum repeaters distribute entanglement over long distances, a prerequisite for the quantum internet. We study its security in a noisy network whose intermediate nodes perform the quantum operations faithfully but may misreport the classical outcome that end users rely on. We show that the ideal functionality of entanglement swapping cannot be realised, because it gives the adversary no influence over the delivered state, whereas a node that relabels its report does have such influence. Including honest noise in the ideal setting does not help unless it also concedes a bounded fraction of misreported pairs. We therefore propose a cut-and-choose protocol that tests a random subset of pairs to certify the rest. Since no test can distinguish a misreport from readout noise, the protocol certifies the error above a pre-agreed minimum noise level, which is the misreport rate under our threat model. This yields a quality-of-service guarantee, in which a failure witnesses that the node did not deliver as agreed. Unlike verification schemes that consume the state they certify, the untested pairs remain available for use, with a finite-size guarantee. Certifying a 1% misreport rate on 10^6 pairs costs 10^4 to 1.7x10^5 test pairs, for minimum noise levels ranging from 0 to 5%. The main cost is memory, since 1% requires holding 10^5 to 10^6 pairs at once, far beyond the capacity of today's quantum memories. Verifying and releasing in blocks ties the memory to the block size at the cost of a weaker rate: a (10^4)-qubit memory certifies 14% on 4.7x10^5 delivered pairs.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling classical hardware for accurate real-time quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26447)
*Chenyang Jin,Susanne F. Yelin,Andi Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Rapids：与 FPGA 协同设计的流式神经解码器，单轮解码<1 μs，距离-19 表面码逻辑错误率较现有实时硬件解码器低 240 倍，且可扩展至颜色码与双变量自行车码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求经典解码器以综合征产生速率实时处理并低延迟返回判决，超导处理器快速综合征周期使解码速度与精度难以兼顾；可跟上速度的解码器牺牲精度，需更大码距与额外物理量子比特补偿。

Method: 提出 Rapids 流式神经网络解码器，与 FPGA 协同设计：网络仅由易映射到硬件的算子构成，并在其整数运算下训练；以单块 FPGA 实现。

Result: 距离-19 表面码单轮解码低于 1 微秒，满足超导处理器需求且延迟低；逻辑错误率比现有实时硬件解码器低 240 倍；同一设计可扩展至颜色码与 [[144,12,12]] 双变量自行车码，精度为实时 Relay 解码器的 16 倍；多 FPGA 分布式或固化为专用硅后，可更快、更准或解码更大码。

Conclusion: 经典硬件成为可与量子比特并列、可扩展以提升容错量子计算机性能的资源。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires a classical decoder fast enough to process syndrome measurements at the rate at which they are generated, and to return its decisions with low latency. This is most demanding for superconducting processors, whose fast syndrome cycles force a trade-off between decoding speed and accuracy. Decoders fast enough to keep up sacrifice accuracy, which must be compensated for with larger codes and thus additional physical qubits. Here we introduce Rapids, a streaming neural decoder that is both fast and accurate. We achieve this by co-designing the decoder with a field-programmable gate array (FPGA): the network is built from operations that map cheaply onto the device and trained in its integer arithmetic. For the distance-19 surface code, Rapids decodes each round of syndromes in under a microsecond on a single FPGA, fast enough for superconducting processors, and returns its decisions with the low latency that fault-tolerant algorithms require. Its logical error rate is 240 times lower than that of existing real-time hardware decoders. The same design extends to the color code and the [[144,12,12]] bivariate-bicycle code, on which our decoder is 16 times more accurate than the real-time Relay decoder. We further show that the decoder scales: spread over several FPGAs or hardened into custom silicon, it becomes faster, more accurate, or able to decode larger codes. Classical hardware thus becomes a further resource, alongside qubits, that can be scaled to improve fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [86] [Optimal Error corrected Hamiltonian estimation with nuisance parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26497)
*Zhiyao Hu,Haidong Yuan,Liang Jiang,Zain H. Saleem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立含扰参量与马尔可夫噪声的量子传感一般框架，给出海森堡标度的充要条件、精确最优精度与可达协议，指出噪声须局域纠错抑制、扰参量不应局域消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子传感同时受噪声与未知扰参量影响；既有马尔可夫动力学下的纠错方案往往对扰参量也做局域抑制，这一做法可能不必要且过度限制。

Method: 针对参数编码于一般含时哈密顿量的情形，在同时存在扰参量与马尔可夫噪声下建立统一框架；推导海森堡标度的充要条件，求解精确最优精度，并构造达到该精度的协议（基于最优设计的相干控制，在全演化过程中分离扰参量累积效应与目标信号）。

Result: 得到海森堡标度的充要条件与精确最优精度及可达协议；表明马尔可夫噪声必须由量子纠错局域抑制，而扰参量无需且一般不应局域消除；即使目标信号瞬时与扰参量方向对齐，仍可实现海森堡标度，这与噪声方向对齐的情形形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 噪声与扰参量施加本质不同的约束、需区别处理；该区分显著扩大了海森堡标度的可实现范围，并为鲁棒量子传感器设计提供一般原则。

Abstract: Realistic quantum sensing is often affected by both noise and unknown nuisance parameters. Under Markovian dynamics, existing error correction approaches have often sought to suppress nuisance responses alongside environmental noise. Here we show that this local suppression of nuisance parameters is unnecessary and can be overly restrictive: nuisance parameters and Markovian noise impose fundamentally different constraints and require distinct treatments. We establish a general framework for quantum sensing with parameters encoded in a general time dependent Hamiltonian, in the presence of both nuisance parameters and Markovian noise. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for Heisenberg scaling, determine the exact optimal precision, and construct protocols that attain it. Our results show that to achieve Heisenberg scaling Markovian noise must be suppressed locally by quantum error correction, whereas nuisance parameters need not, and in general should not, be eliminated locally. Instead, optimally designed coherent control can separate their accumulated effects from the target signal over the full sensing evolution. Consequently, Heisenberg scaling can remain attainable even when the target signal is instantaneously aligned with nuisance parameter directions, in sharp contrast to alignment with Markovian noise. This distinction substantially enlarges the regime in which Heisenberg scaling is attainable and provides a general principle for designing robust quantum sensors.

</details>


### [87] [Hamiltonian Stabilization of the Two-Photon Dicke Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26523)
*Pradip Laha,Kingshuk Adhikary,Subhashish Banerjee,Peter van Loock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 引入正Kac标度数目-Kerr相互作用，使两光子Dicke哈密顿量在任意耦合下都有下界并保持广义Z4对称性，从而获得稳定的一阶相图和成对放大亮相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体两光子光-物质相互作用可实现成对放大，但往往破坏谱限制（spectral confinement），导致无法存在稳定的热力学亮相；本文欲找到一种哈密顿量途径来稳定成对放大。

Method: 在N个发射体的两光子Dicke哈密顿量中加入正的、Kac标度的数目-Kerr项；解析计算零温热力学能量密度（含O(N^{-1})有限尺寸控制）并求解全局相图；结合按对称性分块的精确对角化（至N=128）验证。

Result: Kerr项使哈密顿量在所有耦合下均为下有界且保留广义Z4对称性；亮极小值经鞍结分岔产生，并在共存点与正常相极小值交换全局稳定性，产生光子密度的不连续跃变；精确对角化至N=128支持该密度跃变，并显示同扇区正常-亮相隧穿gap随N指数闭合。

Conclusion: 谱坍缩被重新解释为有限Kerr一阶相图中的奇异零点（Kerr=0点），从而确定了一条稳定成对放大的哈密顿量路线。

Abstract: Collective two-photon light--matter interactions enable pair amplification but can destroy spectral confinement, preventing a stable thermodynamic bright phase. We show that a positive Kac-scaled number-Kerr interaction renders the two-photon Dicke Hamiltonian of \(N\) emitters lower-bounded at all couplings while preserving its generalized \(\mathbb Z_4\) symmetry. We determine the exact zero-temperature thermodynamic energy density with \(O(N^{-1})\) finite-size control and obtain the global phase diagram. A bright minimum is born at a saddle-node and exchanges global stability with the normal minimum at coexistence, producing a discontinuous photon-density jump. Symmetry-resolved exact diagonalization through \(N=128\) supports the predicted density jump and an exponentially closing same-sector normal--bright tunneling gap. Spectral collapse thus becomes the singular zero-Kerr point of a first-order phase diagram, identifying a Hamiltonian route to stabilizing pair amplification.

</details>


### [88] [On the design of sequential quantum repeaters with error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26537)
*Javier Rey-Domínguez,Koji Azuma,Mohsen Razavi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以量子密钥分发为基准，研究中继链上带错误检测的顺序纠缠生成方案的设计选择，发现重复码、量子奇偶码等简单检错码在近期硬件下表现良好，双量子比特重复码可能已能满足多数实际场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离、免信任量子通信依赖可扩展的量子中继方案；带错误检测的顺序纠缠生成兼顾实现难度与逐跳架构兼容性，但其关键设计取舍缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在量子中继链上构建带错误检测的顺序纠缠生成方案，以量子密钥分发作为性能基准，比较不同量子检错码（重复码、量子奇偶码）与无编码策略，并评估不同初始纠缠生成架构。

Result: 简单检错码在近期硬件假设下优于无编码方案，双量子比特重复码在多数实际场景中可能已足够；某些硬件组件的中等改进可部分补偿其他组件进展缓慢的影响。

Conclusion: 简单量子检错码是实用的近期中继方案选择，双量子比特重复码或为多数场景的充分配置；硬件改进间存在可观的补偿效应。

Abstract: Long-distance, trust-free quantum communications rely on the development of scalable quantum repeater schemes. Sequential entanglement generation with error detection is a promising approach that combines manageable implementation requirements with a hop-by-hop architecture compatible with existing classical infrastructure. In this paper, we investigate key design choices for such schemes over a repeater chain using quantum key distribution as a benchmark. We find that simple quantum error-detecting codes, such as repetition codes or quantum parity codes, perform well under near-term hardware assumptions and outperform strategies without any encoding. In particular, we show that two-qubit repetition codes may well be all that we need to implement in many practical scenarios. We also examine different architectures for the generation of initial entanglement and assess their merits. Additionally, we show that moderate improvements in some hardware components can partially mitigate the slow technological progress of others.

</details>


### [89] [Authenticating quantum key distribution through random position checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26542)
*Florian J. Curchod,Erik Woodhead,Cameron Foreman,Marcel Mordarski,Harry Buhrman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出将一方位置的量子位置验证（QPV）轮随机插入BB84流程，用位置作为认证凭据，效率高于每比特跑一次完整QPV的旧方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD必须依赖认证，以防止一方在不知情下与冒充诚实方的对手生成密钥；而现有将QPV用于认证的方法开销大（每个需认证比特都要运行完整QPV协议），效率低。

Method: 协议由BB84 QKD与量子位置验证轮随机交错构成，以其中一方的位置作为密钥分发的认证凭据；并对受限攻击集合进行初步安全性分析。

Result: 相比此前方法效率显著提升；在限定攻击模型下给出了初始安全分析结论。

Conclusion: 位置可作为QKD的认证凭据，交错式BB84+QPV设计具备效率优势，但安全性仅在受限攻击范围内得到初步论证，完整安全证明留待后续工作。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) requires some form of authentication in order for the parties to be sure they are not unknowingly generating a key with an adversary pretending to be one of the honest parties. In this work, we propose a protocol in which the position of one of the parties is used as the authentication credential for key distribution. Our protocol essentially consists of a BB84 QKD protocol with quantum position verification (QPV) rounds randomly interleaved in. The approach is efficient compared with previous approaches, which required running an entire QPV protocol for every bit requiring authentication. We undertake an initial security analysis of the protocol for a restricted set of attacks.

</details>


### [90] [Classical Oracle Separation of QMA from the QCMA Hierarchy and Quantum Cryptography Even When P = QCMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26574)
*Alper Cakan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 作者引入"噪声中的码字-哈希对"问题（CHN），在查询复杂度模型下证明其属于 QMA 但对 QCMAH 困难，由此构造经典 oracle 使 P=BQP=QCMA=QCMAH 严格包含于 QMA，并在 P=QCMA 的世界中构造多种量子密码原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Cakan 为分离 QMA 与 QCIP 而提出的 Codeword-Hash Structure 问题启发，作者希望刻画"量子证明/量子态相对经典证明的优越性"能在多弱的假设下存在——即便 P=BQP=QCMA（即既无量子可计算单向函数也无可验证量子计算优势）时，量子密码与量子证明优势是否仍可能存在；同时回答 Kretschmer–Qian–Tal（STOC 2025）的开放问题。

Method: 提出新问题 CHN（Codeword-Hash pairs in Noise），在查询复杂度模型下给出其属于 QMA 的证明，并证明其对 QCMA 层级 QCMAH = ∪_d QCMA^{QCMA^{…}} 困难；借助该问题构造经典 oracle O，采用相对化/ oracle 分离技术实现 P^O=BQP^O=QCMA^O=QCMAH^O ⊊ QMA^O 且 QMA^O ⊄ QCMAH^O 的分离。

Result: 得到经典 oracle O 使 P^O=BQP^O=QCMA^O=QCMAH^O ⊂ QMA^O 但 QMA^O ⊄ QCMAH^O；并证明在 P=QCMA 且不存在量子单向函数与（可验证）量子计算优势的世界中，仍可在该 oracle 下构造经典密钥的伪随机态、具有可复制保护量子解密密钥且公钥与密文均为经典的公钥加密等原语。

Conclusion: 量子态/量子证明的优势可以与经典验证能力完全解耦：即使经典计算机能高效求解一切"经典证明可量子验证"的问题，量子证明优势依然存在；量子密码原语的存在性不依赖于量子单向函数或量子计算优势，从而回答了 Kretschmer–Qian–Tal（STOC 2025）的开放问题。

Abstract: Remark: This work is in submission at STOC'27 and thus the full manuscript has been publicly posted on the conference website since November 3rd, 2026.
  Inspired by the Codeword-Hash Structure problem recently introduced by Cakan (arXiV, 2026) to separate $\mathsf{QMA}$ from $\mathsf{QCIP}$, we introduce a new problem called Codeword-Hash pairs in Noise ($\mathsf{CHN}$) and prove that, in the query complexity model, it is in $\mathsf{QMA}$ but hard for the $\mathsf{QCMA}$ hierarchy $\mathsf{QCMAH} = \bigcup_{d\geq1}\mathsf{QCMA}^{\mathsf{QCMA}^{\cdots^{\mathsf{QCMA}}}}$, the classical-witness quantum-verifier analogue of $\mathsf{PH}=\bigcup_{d\ge1}\mathsf{NP}^{\mathsf{NP}^{\cdots^{\mathsf{NP}}}}$.
  Using this problem, we show that there exists a classical oracle ${O}$ such that $\mathsf{P}^{O}=\mathsf{BQP}^{O}=\mathsf{QCMA}^{O} =\mathsf{QCMAH}^{O} \subset \mathsf{QMA}^{O} \text{ but } \mathsf{QMA}^{O}\not\subseteq\mathsf{QCMAH}^{O},$ thus showing that quantum state or quantum proof advantage could exist even if classical computers can efficiently solve any problem that can be quantumly verified with a classical proof.
  Further, using our problem, we show that various quantum cryptographic primitives can exist even when $\mathsf{P} = \mathsf{QCMA}$ and thus neither quantum-computable one-way functions nor (verifiable) quantum computational advantage exist. In particular, relative to a classical oracle ${O}$ with $\mathsf{P}^{O}=\mathsf{BQP}^{O}=\mathsf{QCMA}^{O}=\mathsf{QCMAH}^{O}$, there exist pseudorandom states with classical keys and public-key encryption with copy-protected quantum decryption keys and classical public keys and ciphertexts, among other primitives, answering an open question of Kretschmer, Qian, and Tal (STOC 2025).

</details>


### [91] [Classically Trained Quantum Generative Models with Shared Training and Fine Tuning for Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26577)
*Mario Herrero-Gonzalez,Elham Kashefi,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用超网络在哈密顿量参数→电路角度的映射上共享相图信息，配合无偏蒙特卡洛傅里叶系数估计器与CDF-KL损失，在Haldane与横场Ising模型上实现更少局部更新、更低误差的量子采样器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态测量统计刻画量子物质，但直接采样完整分布困难；而选定的关联量可用于引导量子采样器的经典训练。作者希望验证：跨相关基态分布进行共享训练，能否让采样器更准确地复现这些关联量。

Method: 训练超网络将哈密顿量参数映射为电路角度，在相图上共享信息，随后用额外参考数据做局部微调以供量子部署；推导针对一般Pauli旋转电路的傅里叶系数无偏蒙特卡洛估计器，代价取决于电路生成元；引入CDF-KL损失比较选定边缘分布；在Haldane模型与横场Ising模型上，用自动搜索在相变附近选取参考计算。

Result: 十二站点链上，自适应预训练超网络以更少局部更新即达到独立拟合的最终平均全变分距离，并继续收敛到更低误差；在1,024站点链与4×256格点上，微调电路相对参考分布的平方最大均值差异在各几何的评估点上均低于独立训练电路。

Conclusion: 结果支持在相图范围内共享训练以制备量子采样器，并辅以局部微调的策略。

Abstract: Ground-state measurement statistics characterize quantum matter. Selected correlations can guide the classical training of a quantum sampler even when sampling the full distribution is difficult. We ask whether training across related ground-state distributions helps the sampler reproduce their correlations more accurately. We train a hypernetwork to map Hamiltonian parameters to circuit angles, sharing information across the phase diagram before local fine-tuning with additional reference data for quantum deployment. For classical training, we derive an unbiased Monte Carlo estimator of Fourier coefficients for circuits built from general Pauli rotations, with a cost that depends on the circuit generators. We also introduce a CDF-KL loss for comparing selected marginals. We study the Haldane Hamiltonian and transverse-field Ising model, using an automated search to select reference calculations near phase transitions. On the twelve-site chain, the adaptively pretrained hypernetwork matches the independent fits' final mean total variation distance in fewer local updates and continues to a lower error. On a 1,024-site chain and a 4 by 256 lattice, the fine-tuned circuits have lower squared maximum mean discrepancy from the reference distributions than the independent circuits at the evaluated point in each geometry. These results motivate shared training for preparing quantum samplers across a phase diagram and local fine-tuning.

</details>


### [92] [End-to-End Quantum Algorithm for Differential Equations: Application to Simulating a Biomolecular System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26582)
*Mohsen Bagherimehrab,Daniyaal Farooqi,Gavin K. Brennen,Barry C. Sanders,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向实际应用偏微分方程的端到端量子算法：针对线性化Poisson-Boltzmann方程，完整实现从经典输入到经典输出的全流程，电路规模对网格点数与精度均为多对数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子微分方程算法依赖抽象oracle与理想化假设，其实际可行性与计算优势不明确，缺乏从经典输入到经典输出的完整实现。

Method: 将端到端量子算法形式化为接受经典输入、返回经典输出；为构建该算法的各基本原语给出具体实现；针对变系数线性化Poisson-Boltzmann方程，提出网格上变系数PDE的新离散表示以高效实现算子oracle，并结合最优小波预条件技术生成与离散算子条件数无关的量子解；显式实现从初态制备、量子求解到性质估计的全流程；给出电路规模与经典预处理的复杂度分析。

Result: 算法可由规模对网格点数N_g与精度倒数1/ε均为多对数的量子电路实现，总门成本对N_g为多对数、对1/ε为准线性；构造电路的经典预处理成本同样对N_g和1/ε为多对数；相对经典方法在N_g上实现指数级改进。

Conclusion: 工作给出了一个具有实际价值的PDE端到端量子算法，展示了相对经典方法的指数级扩展优势，并为评估其实际量子优势奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for differential equations have the potential for substantial algorithmic speedups over classical methods. However, they rely on certain assumptions and abstract oracle specifications that leave their practical feasibility and computational advantages for realistic applications unclear. Here we formalize the concept of an end-to-end quantum algorithm for differential equations as one that accepts classical inputs and returns classical outputs, and provide implementations for algorithmic primitives in constructing such algorithms. We present a tailored end-to-end quantum algorithm for the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann (LPB) equation, a partial differential equation (PDE) with variable coefficients widely used in simulating a biomolecular system. We explicitly implement all aspects of the algorithm, from preparing an initial state to generating a quantum solution and estimating properties of the system. In particular, we develop a new discrete representation for variable-coefficient PDEs on a grid that enables efficient implementation of the operator oracle, and build on a recent technique based on optimal wavelet preconditioning to generate a quantum solution with a cost independent of the condition number of the discretized operator. We show the algorithm can be implemented by quantum circuits of size polylogarithmic in both the number of grid points $N_g$ and the inverse of the desired accuracy $\varepsilon$, with an overall gate cost polylogarithmic in $N_g$ and quasilinear in $1/\varepsilon$. We also show the classical preprocessing to construct the circuits has polylogarithmic cost in $N_g$ and $1/\varepsilon$. Our work provides a concrete end-to-end quantum algorithm for a PDE of practical value, demonstrates an exponential improvement in scaling with $N_g$ over classical methods, and provides a foundation for assessing its potential quantum advantage in practice.

</details>


### [93] [Fast, in-place, and ancilla-free quantum algorithms for integer multiplication and inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26605)
*David Halpenny,Adithya Sireesh,Petros Wallden*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出若干原地量子算术运算算法，在门数与辅助比特开销上优于现有最优方案：2的幂模下的原地模乘、原地整数乘法与原地模逆均可在 \(O(n^{1+\varepsilon})\) 门内完成，且多数运算无需辅助比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术运算是量子算法的基础构件，其实现成本直接影响大量量子应用的可行性；现有原地算术方案在门数（二次级）或辅助比特开销上存在瓶颈，需要同时降低门数与空间开销。

Method: 以统一的定理框架推导乘法算法：(1) 模 \(2^n\) 的原地模乘，支持经典或量子奇乘数，代价 \(O(n^{1+\varepsilon})\) 门、零辅助比特；(2) 标准原地整数乘法，非零经典乘数需零辅助比特，量子乘数仅需对数个辅助比特；(3) 基于模乘算法构造模 \(2^n\) 的原地模逆，同等渐近代价。核心在于用递归/分块式归约将问题拆解，从而避免二次级门数并压低辅助空间。

Result: 对所有考虑到的原地运算，首次同时实现亚二次门数与亚线性辅助比特数；除一项外，所有快速运算在内存上达到最优，仅需存放输出所需空间并直接覆写输入。

Conclusion: 给出了一组高空间效率、低门数开销的原地量子算术算法，其统一定理形式可能进一步应用于其他量子算术问题。

Abstract: Arithmetic operations are a fundamental building block in the design of quantum algorithms, with their implementation cost expected to have a significant impact on the viability of a wide variety of quantum applications. In this work, we present novel algorithms for a number of in-place quantum arithmetic operations that are highly efficient in terms of their space and gate count overheads compared to the current state-of-the-art. We show that in-place modular multiplication of integers, modulo a power of two, can be performed with either a classical or quantum odd multiplier using $O(n^{1+ε})$ gates and no ancilla qubits, for any $ε>0$. We then show that standard in-place integer multiplication can be performed with a non-zero classical or quantum multiplier using $O(n^{1+ε})$ gates for any $ε>0$, with no ancilla qubits required for the classical multiplier and a logarithmic number of ancilla qubits for the quantum. Using our quantum modular multiplication algorithm, we also show that in-place modular inversion, modulo a power of two, can be performed using $O(n^{1+ε})$ gates and no ancilla qubits, for any $ε>0$. To our knowledge, these are the first quantum algorithms achieving both subquadratic gate counts and sublinear ancilla counts for each of the considered in-place operations. All but one of these fast operations are optimal in terms of memory, only requiring enough space to store the output of the operation, which overwrites the input directly. We present our multiplication algorithms as consequences of a single unified theorem, which may find further application within quantum arithmetic.

</details>


### [94] [Query-Optimal Quantum Minimum Finding from Numerical Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26606)
*Egil Bae*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数值估计器的量子最小查找算法，避免搜索前对估计置信度放大带来的对数查询开销；在常数置信度下用 O(√N) 次基础估计器查询即可找到 2ε-最优标签，且查询复杂度与加性误差常数分别达到最坏情况最优；更一般地，对"以已知概率 p 命中目标值不超过 a"的制备过程，无需知道 a 或分布即可返回候选标签及其 ε-精确估计 v，并满足 v≤a+ε（常数概率联合成立），最坏情况下对制备与估计预言机各用 O(p^{-1/2}) 次查询，候选可作为量子态保留；量子比特数为 O(s_P+w+log log(4/p))，与目标置信度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子最小查找需先对数值估计器的估计做振幅放大/置信度放大，引入 log 因子查询开销；本文旨在消除该对数开销，并在未知目标值阈值与分布的情况下给出更一般的、可保留量子候选态的框架。

Method: 构造相干估计器（每标签 ε-精确概率≥2/3），结合量子最小查找，在搜索过程中直接使用低置信度估计而非预先放大；进一步推广到仅已知"概率质量 ≥p 对应目标值 ≤a"的制备模型，算法无需知道 a 与分布，仅调用制备预言机与估计预言机，并以 O(s_P+w+log log(4/p)) 量子比特实现（与请求置信度无关）。

Result: 常数置信度下 O(√N) 次基础估计器查询得到 2ε-最优标签，查询上界与加性误差常数分别最坏情况最优；一般情形下 O(p^{-1/2}) 次制备/估计查询、O(s_P+w+log log(4/p)) 量子比特；应用于有界期望的 η-最优选择达到最坏情况最优的 Θ(√N/η)；引导基态能量估计以 O(ω^{-1}) 次制备和 O((ωη)^{-1}) 次基本受控演化查询（常数置信度）。

Conclusion: 无需预先放大估计置信度即可实现查询最优的量子最小查找，且框架推广到阈值未知、分布未知的概率制备场景，同时保持候选的量子态形式与低量子比特开销，为期望估计、基态能量估计等提供最坏情况最优的查询复杂度。

Abstract: We propose a quantum minimum-finding algorithm for objectives accessed via numerical estimators that avoids the logarithmic query overhead of amplifying the confidence of estimates before the search. A coherent estimator that is $\varepsilon$-accurate with probability at least $2/3$ on each of $N$ labels suffices to find a $2\varepsilon$-optimal label using $O(\sqrt N)$ base-estimator queries at constant confidence. The query bound and the additive-error constant are separately worst-case optimal. More generally, suppose a preparation assigns probability mass at least a known $p$ to objective values at most $a$. Without knowing $a$ or the prepared distribution, our algorithm returns a candidate and an estimate $v$ such that, jointly with constant probability, $v$ is $\varepsilon$-accurate for the candidate and $v\le a+\varepsilon$. The algorithm uses $O(p^{-1/2})$ queries to each of the preparation and estimator oracles in the worst case, and the selected candidate can be retained as a quantum state. For preparation width $s_P$ and estimator workspace width $w$, it requires $O(s_P+w+\log\log(4/p))$ qubits, independently of the requested confidence. Applications give worst-case optimal $Θ(\sqrt N/η)$ queries for selecting an $η$-optimal bounded expectation, and guided ground-energy estimation using $O(ω^{-1})$ preparations and $O((ωη)^{-1})$ elementary controlled-evolution queries at constant confidence, where $ω$ is a lower bound on the guiding state's ground-space amplitude overlap.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Dynamical Variational Theory of Stochastic Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25491)
*Moshir Harsh,Peter Sollich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于场论表示与Plefka展开，提出动态变分近似方法，用于离散随机反应网络的均值、关联、响应函数与稳态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散随机种群动力学（生化反应网络、基因调控、传染病、生态与群体遗传学）中有限种群造成强内禀涨落，而化学主方程无一般解析解、数值法随体系规模扩展性差、标准近似在低拷贝数下失效，亟需统一的分析框架。

Method: 采用反应动力学的场论表示，借鉴自旋玻璃物理中的Plefka展开，构建随机反应网络的动力学变分近似；对具一般化学计量与含时速率的系统导出含自洽记忆的可处理非马尔可夫有效动力学，并引入广义自洽气泡重求和给出均值、关联与响应函数的闭合预测；提供自适应时间步长的数值实现。

Result: 在随机基因调控、酶动力学与感染动力学基准上，该方法同时准确刻画瞬态行为与稳态，并能捕捉传统近似遗漏的涨落驱动效应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了适用于强涨落低拷贝数系统、覆盖瞬态与稳态区域的随机反应动力学通用解析框架。

Abstract: Discrete stochastic population dynamics describe systems ranging from biochemical reaction networks and gene regulation to epidemic spreading, ecology, and population genetics, where finite populations generate strong intrinsic fluctuations. Predicting their dynamics, including mean populations, fluctuations, time-lagged correlations, response functions, and steady states, remains challenging: the chemical master equation admits no general analytical solution, numerical solutions scale poorly with system size, and standard approximations fail at low copy numbers. Using a field-theoretic representation of the reaction dynamics, we develop a dynamical variational approximation for stochastic reaction networks based on the Plefka expansion from spin-glass physics. For reaction systems with general stoichiometry and time-dependent rates, the expansion yields tractable non-Markovian effective dynamics with self-consistent memory. We further introduce a generalized self-consistent bubble resummation that provides closed predictions for means, correlations, and response functions. The formulation applies broadly to discrete stochastic population dynamics on networks, and we provide a numerical implementation with adaptive time stepping for user-defined systems. We benchmark the method on stochastic gene regulation, enzyme kinetics, and infection dynamics, where it accurately captures both time-dependent behaviour and steady states, including fluctuation-driven effects missed by conventional approximations. Together, these results establish a general analytical framework for stochastic reaction dynamics across transient and stationary regimes in strongly fluctuating low-copy-number systems.

</details>


### [96] [Critical droplets and metastability in long-range chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25764)
*Laura Batini,Valerio Pagni,Nicolò Defenu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究一维长程Ising链在一阶相变中的亚稳衰变，发现临界液滴半径R_c∝h^{-1/σ}，且σ=1为两类行为的临界分界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻Ising链中液滴两侧壁面代价与尺寸无关，该经典图像在长程相互作用下是否成立、以及核化势垒如何随偏置场h标度，尚不清楚。

Method: 采用衰减为1/r^{1+σ}的长程相互作用一维Ising链模型，分析表面代价与体自由能增益的竞争；并用非局域φ^4场论求解具有代数尾的临界液滴，辅以随机长程动力学模拟独立检验。

Result: 小h下临界半径满足R_c∝h^{-1/σ}；σ<1时激活势垒在h→0^+时发散，σ=1时呈对数增长，σ>1时保持有限（与最近邻情形一致）。非局域φ^4场论确认半径与势垒标度律，随机长程动力学独立复现临界半径标度。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用使液滴表面代价依赖尺寸，从而改变一维核化的普适行为；σ=1为非平庸相变点，划分势垒发散、对数增长与有限三类标度机制。

Abstract: Metastable decay in first-order phase transitions proceeds through the nucleation of critical droplets. In the one-dimensional nearest-neighbor Ising chain, the cost of the two walls bounding a droplet is independent of its size. For interactions decaying as $1/r^{1+σ}$, this surface cost instead depends on the droplet size and competes with the bulk free-energy gain. The critical radius scales as $R_c\propto h^{-1/σ}$ at small $h$, where $h>0$ is the bias field. The value $σ=1$ separates two regimes: for $σ<1$, the activation barrier diverges as $h\to0^+$; at $σ=1$, it grows logarithmically; and for $σ>1$, it remains finite, as in the nearest-neighbor chain. A nonlocal $φ^4$ field theory yields critical droplets with algebraic tails and confirms the predicted radius and barrier scalings, while stochastic long-range dynamics independently reproduces the critical-radius scaling.

</details>


### [97] [Chasing the KPZ exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26166)
*Luciano C. Lapas,Márcio S. Gomes-Filho,Fernando A. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该Perspective指出，即便缺乏精确解，将KPZ非线性视为作用于基底分形子集上的有效噪声，可给出粗糙度指数的严格界 1/(d+1) ≤ α ≤ 1/d，并用四十年的理论、模拟与实验检验其假设与可证伪判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ方程写入文献四十年后，一维情形已获精确解并被精密实验证实，二维极化激元凝聚体中亦观测到其标度行为；但在一维以上，尚无任何一个指数被精确确定，甚至连上临界维是否存在仍有争议。作者认为，对KPZ普适类的可靠结论不必等待精确解。

Method: 将KPZ方程中的非线性项视为作用在基底一个分形子集上的有效噪声，据此推导d维空间中粗糙度指数α的上下界：1/(d+1) ≤ α ≤ 1/d。文中系统检视该推导所依赖的假设（有效噪声图像、分形维数选取、标度假设等），并将其与四十年来的理论（微扰重整化群、函数重整化群、数值模拟）以及最新实验结果相对照。

Result: 得到粗糙度指数的通用界 1/(d+1) ≤ α ≤ 1/d；文中梳理了该界与既有理论、数值模拟和实验（含二维极化激元凝聚体）的一致或冲突之处，并指出在三维修及以上维度进行检验是判定该界成立与否的关键。

Conclusion: 对KPZ标度，即使没有精确解也可给出严格的指数界与可证伪预言；关键在于明确界定这些界所依赖的假设，并通过三维及以上维度的针对性检验（理论与数值/实验）来确立或否定它们。

Abstract: Forty years after it was written down, the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) equation is the paradigm of scale invariance far from equilibrium. In one dimension it has been solved exactly and confirmed by precision experiments, and its scaling has now been observed in two-dimensional polariton condensates. Above one dimension, however, not a single exponent is known exactly, and even the existence of an upper critical dimension is disputed. In this Perspective we argue that firm statements need not wait for exact solutions. Treating the nonlinearity as an effective noise that acts on a fractal subset of the substrate leads to bounds on the roughness exponent, $1/(d+1)\leα\le 1/d$ in $d$ dimensions. We examine the assumptions behind these bounds, confront them with four decades of theory, simulations and the new experiments, and identify the tests---foremost in three and more dimensions---that would establish or refute them.

</details>


### [98] [Wigner-Dyson correlations in selected sectors of an integrable spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26263)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 可积自旋链在开边界下呈指数简并，周期边界下少数全局对称扇区出现Wigner-Dyson统计，作者归因于非局域Hilbert空间碎裂，并认为动力学并不混沌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner-Dyson谱统计通常被视为量子混沌的标志，而可积模型一般服从Poisson统计。作者希望考察可积自旋链在标准Yang-Baxter框架内是否会出现反常谱关联，并厘清谱统计与动力学混沌之间的判据关系。

Method: 构造一类干净的、平移不变的可积自旋链（可嵌入标准Yang-Baxter框架），分别在开边界与周期边界条件下数值分析能谱统计，并按全局对称性分扇区考察；结合Hilbert空间碎裂（非局域型）解释谱结构。

Result: 开边界下模型具有均匀的指数级简并；周期边界下简并被劈裂，且由全局对称性选出的少数扇区表现出Wigner-Dyson谱统计；两种边界条件下碎裂程度不同，碎裂为非局域型。

Conclusion: Wigner-Dyson谱关联在此并不等价于量子混沌：该模型虽可积且谱统计反常，但其动力学并不混沌，Hilbert空间碎裂是支配谱性质的关键机制，边界条件通过改变碎裂范围调控谱统计。

Abstract: In quantum many-body physics spectral statistics is often used as a probe to distinguish integrable and chaotic models. We find a special case of an integrable spin chain with unusual spectral properties. With open boundary conditions the model has homogeneous exponential degeneracies; these are split for periodic boundary conditions. In the latter case, we find that a small number of sectors (selected by a global symmetry) showcase Wigner-Dyson spectral statistics, which usually signals quantum chaos. Our model is clean, translationally invariant, and it fits into the standard Yang-Baxter framework. We identify Hilbert space fragmentation as the key mechanism behind the spectral properties. The fragmentation is non-local, and its extent is different in the case of open and periodic boundary conditions. We argue that the dynamics of the model is not chaotic despite the unusual spectral correlations.

</details>


### [99] [Exact finite-time fluctuation-response relations for Markov processes: A martingale framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26267)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出以Doob鞅为核心的鞅框架，为一般马尔可夫过程的可加可观测量给出精确有限时间的涨落-响应恒等式，并由此导出多种不等式、变分原理与频域界，应用于欠阻尼动力学得到摩擦-响应TUR及熵产生变分原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分可观测量的涨落与响应均可实验测量，其之间的关系能够揭示维持非平衡态的耗散，但缺乏对一般马尔可夫过程精确、有限时间的统一描述框架。

Method: 构建鞅框架：用Doob鞅给出可观测量的方差精确恒等式，将总方差分解为初始系综继承部分与观测期间噪声产生部分；证明在固定制备下，观测量均值对任意容许扰动的响应等于Doob鞅与表示扰动的第二鞅之间的协方差；对扩散与跳跃过程，单一鞅幅度同时决定涨落与响应；进而导出不等式、变分原理及多变量与频域界。

Result: 得到可在任意有限时间饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定性关系（TUR），并给出熵产生的变分原理；证明速度分辨流即使在线性高斯动力学中也能指数级强违反传统TUR；推出极限环的弱噪声耗散-相干性界，表明惯性振子只有在频率对摩擦弱敏感时才能超越过阻尼界；对过阻尼动力学与跳跃过程复现并推广了近期涨落-响应恒等式，并识别不确定性关系饱和的条件。

Conclusion: Doob鞅方法为涨落-响应关系提供了精确、统一的有限时间描述，统一涵盖扩散、跳跃与欠阻尼/过阻尼动力学，并澄清TUR的饱和与违反机制及惯性振子的优势条件。

Abstract: Fluctuations and responses of time-integrated observables are both experimentally measurable, and relations between them can reveal the dissipation that sustains a nonequilibrium state. Here we develop a martingale framework that gives exact finite-time fluctuation-response identities for additive observables of general Markov processes. We obtain an exact identity for the variance of an observable in terms of its Doob martingale, which splits the total variance into a part inherited from the initial ensemble and a part generated by noise during the observation. We show that, at fixed preparation, the response of the mean of the observable to any admissible perturbation is the covariance between the Doob martingale and a second martingale that represents the perturbation. For diffusions and jump processes, a single martingale amplitude determines both the fluctuations and the response of the observable. These identities yield inequalities, variational principles, and multivariate and frequency-domain bounds, whose costs are set by the corresponding physical perturbations. Applying the framework to underdamped dynamics, we obtain a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) that can be saturated at any finite time and thus gives a variational principle for the entropy production. We also demonstrate exponentially strong violations of the conventional TUR by velocity-resolved currents, even in linear Gaussian dynamics, and derive a weak-noise dissipation-coherence bound for limit cycles, which shows that inertial oscillators can beat the overdamped bound only if their frequency is weakly sensitive to friction. For overdamped dynamics and jump processes, the framework recovers and extends recent fluctuation-response identities and identifies when the corresponding uncertainty relations are saturated.

</details>


### [100] [First-passage and correlated reset-count statistics under threshold and Poissonian resetting in a two-dimensional wedge geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26345)
*Fazil Najeeb,Arnab Pal,V. V Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究二维开口楔形域中阈值重置(TR)与泊松重置对布朗目标搜索的竞争与协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单一重置机制，而实际搜索过程中多种重置（如碰到边界触发重置与随机时间重置）可能并存，其相互作用尚不清晰，需要建立统一框架刻画两种重置竞争下的首达时间统计与计数统计。

Method: 以顶点角α的二维开口楔形域为几何背景，零角边界为吸收靶，α边界触发阈值重置。先用Biswas等(PRL 135, 227101, 2025)的更新(renewal)框架求TR单独作用时FPT的一、二阶矩；再发展基于随机变量的通用更新形式推导两种重置联合的平均首达时间(MFPT)；并推导两种重置机制的联合计数统计，用Pearson相关系数量化其相互依赖。

Result: TR单独作用时FPT密度呈代数长时尾 t^{-1-π/(2α)}；联合MFPT显示：当0<α<π/4时，由TR动力学的变异系数决定泊松重置加速还是阻碍搜索，而α≥π/4时泊松重置总是降低MFPT；计数统计显示随泊松重置率变化出现由弱到强的相关性交叉，且交叉行为对初始搜索位置有非平凡依赖。

Conclusion: 建立了统一处理竞争性重置机制的分析框架，揭示了楔形角与变异系数对重置效果的判据作用，并为更一般几何中的多重重置研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the interplay between threshold resetting (TR) and Poissonian resetting in a Brownian target search problem confined to a two-dimensional open wedge of apex angle $α$. The boundary at zero angle acts as an absorbing target, while encounters with the boundary at $α$ trigger TR, instantaneously restarting the search from its initial position. For TR alone, using the renewal framework developed by Biswas et al. in Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 227101 (2025), we obtain exact first and second moments of the first-passage time (FPT), whose density exhibits an algebraic long-time tail $t^{-1-\fracπ{2α}}$. To combine the two resetting mechanisms, we thereafter develop a generic random-variable-based renewal formalism to derive the combined mean FPT. We show that the coefficient of variation of the underlying TR dynamics determines whether Poissonian resetting accelerates or hinders the search for $0<α<π/4$, whereas for $α\geqπ/4$, Poissonian resetting always reduces the MFPT. Furthermore, we derive the joint count statistics of the two resetting mechanisms and characterize their mutual dependence through Pearson's correlation coefficient. Interestingly, we uncover a crossover from weak to strong correlations as the Poissonian resetting rate is varied, with the crossover behavior exhibiting a nontrivial dependence on the initial search position. Our framework provides a basis for studying competing resetting mechanisms in more general geometries.

</details>


### [101] [Imaginary-Time Density Correlations Encode Quantum Entropy and Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26381)
*Sagi Meir*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于虚时密度关联与相对熵推断的量子超额熵闭式估计，将经典最大熵关系推广到量子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最大熵推断可由静态结构因子的单个动量求和估计超额熵，但量子体系中相互作用改变动能、离域与交换带来等时关联不含的信息，使该估计失效。

Method: 以理想玻色/费米气体为参考，对虚时路径做相对熵（最大熵）推断，在与经典关系相同的高斯近似下导出超额熵与自由能的闭式表达；输入仅为单态点上的虚时密度关联及其理想气体参考，不使用相互作用势。数据可来自路径积分模拟与X射线汤姆孙散射。

Result: 闭式在经典极限下还原经典结果，并能给出经典关系遗漏的熵；在弱相互作用玻色子、环上相互作用费米子及温稠密电子气上验证有效。

Conclusion: 虚时密度关联可承载量子超额熵信息，使经典熵估计框架可系统推广至量子体系。

Abstract: In the classical limit, maximum-entropy inference yields an estimate of the excess entropy from the static structure factor through a single momentum sum. Quantum mechanically this estimate fails, because interactions also change the kinetic energy, while delocalization and exchange add information that equal-time correlations do not contain. We show that imaginary-time density correlations contain this information, and that with them the classical relation carries over to quantum systems. Such correlations are available from path-integral simulations and X-ray Thomson scattering. Relative-entropy inference over imaginary-time paths, with an ideal Bose or Fermi gas as reference, gives closed forms for the excess entropy and free energies, within the same Gaussian approximation as the classical relation. They recover the classical result and estimate entropy that the classical relation misses. We test them on weakly interacting bosons, interacting fermions on a ring, and the warm dense electron gas. The closed forms need only imaginary-time density correlations and their ideal-gas reference at a single state point, without using the interaction potential. Our approach can extend other classical entropy estimators to quantum systems.

</details>


### [102] [Nonequilibrium Statistical Mechanics: Physical Concepts & Mathematical Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26394)
*Sagar Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 作为面向复杂系统的入门讲义，本文梳理了经典非平衡统计力学的基本概念与方法，强调其跨学科应用，作者仅作综合与呈现而不宣称原创性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡统计力学缺乏公认的完整主题清单（凡不在平衡态者皆属非平衡），但已超越传统物理学，成为描述复杂系统的通用语言；作者的讲授热情与对复杂系统的好奇推动其撰写入门材料。

Method: 教学性综述/讲义：综合并系统呈现经典非平衡统计力学的若干入门概念与技术，假定读者已具备平衡态统计力学基础。

Result: 未给出具体研究结果；文本为讲义导言，说明所涉概念与方法在基础物理之外的生物学、生态学、经济学和社会学等领域均有成功应用。

Conclusion: 这些讲义旨在为跨学科读者提供非平衡统计力学的入门训练，除材料的特定综合与呈现方式外，作者不主张任何原创性。

Abstract: As anything not in equilibrium is, by definition, nonequilibrium, there is no universally agreed-upon set of topics that can constitute an exhaustive presentation of nonequilibrium statistical mechanics. At the same time, nonequilibrium statistical mechanics has transcended traditional physics to become a powerful language for complex systems. Prompted by an enthusiastic curiosity about complex systems, these cursory lecture notes attempt to elucidate some introductory concepts and techniques used in classical nonequilibrium statistical mechanics, which find successful applications not only in foundational physics, but also across other fields such as biology, ecology, economics, and sociology. Except for the specific synthesis and presentation of the material, which assumes basic familiarity with equilibrium statistical mechanics, the author claims no originality.

</details>


### [103] [Exact fermionic decay length and Majorana localization in the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26529)
*Shahane Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara SEdrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型在 p=π/2 处存在精确费米子衰減长度 ξ_f^{-1}=|ln(U/|V|)|，其发散点 V=±|U| 对应两条经典临界分支，并分别映射为横场 Ising 链在 0、π 处的能隙闭合及 Majorana 端模局域化长度；同时证明 Yang--Baxter 族在该拟设下不可延拓至有限耦合内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型中，费米子衰減长度的精确形式及其与临界分支、拓扑端模的联系尚缺乏严格刻画。

Method: 取固定横向动量 p=π/2，以 x=tanh K1、y=tanh K2、z=tanh K3 表述耦合，通过 Jordan--Wigner 变换得到费米子链，并计算每晶胞逆衰減长度 ξ_f^{-1}=|ln(U/|V|)|，其中 U=(1-x²)(1+y²z²)、V=(1+x²)(y²-z²)；在强弱键同时标度下与行转移矩阵所得横场 Ising 链比较，并分析 Yang--Baxter 族的可延拓性。

Result: 衰減长度在 U=|V| 处发散，V 的符号对应两条经典临界分支；在强弱键同时标度极限下，这两支分别化为横场 Ising 链在动量 0 与 π 的能隙闭合条件；经典衰減长度趋于 Jordan--Wigner 费米子链 Majorana 端模的局域化长度。

Conclusion: 给出符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型费米子衰減长度的精确表达式，将其临界行为与横场 Ising 链的能隙闭合、Majorana 端模局域化统一起来；并证明在固定基 avec 一个键方向消失的拟设下，正则强键 Yang--Baxter 族无法延拓至有限耦合内部。

Abstract: We derive an exact fermionic decay length for the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model at fixed transverse momentum $p=π/2$. Writing $x=\tanh K_1$, $y=\tanh K_2$, and $z=\tanh K_3$ for the three dimensionless couplings, the inverse length per lattice cell is $ξ_{\rm f}^{-1}=|\ln(U/|V|)|$, where $U=(1-x^2)(1+y^2z^2)$ and $V=(1+x^2)(y^2-z^2)$. The length diverges at $U=|V|$, with the sign of $V$ selecting one of two classical critical branches. In a simultaneous strong- and weak-bond scaling, these branches become the gap-closing conditions at momenta $0$ and $π$ of the transverse-field Ising chain obtained from the row transfer matrix. On the topological side, the classical decay length tends to the localization length of a Majorana end mode of the Jordan--Wigner fermion chain. We also prove that the regular strong-bond Yang--Baxter family cannot be continued to the finite-coupling interior within the fixed-basis ansatz with one vanishing bond direction.

</details>


### [104] [Field theory and the biquadratic spin-1 chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26610)
*Luisa Eck,Paul Fendley*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用Bethe ansatz与保持可积性的标度极限，论证反铁磁双线性自旋-1链的低能激发是去禁闭畴壁，其极限由非相对论可积场论描述，而非相对论$\mathbb{CP}^2$模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准论证认为反铁磁双二次自旋-1链的低能物理由$\theta=\pi$的$\mathbb{CP}^2$非线性sigma模型描述，但链本身可积而该场论一般认为不可积，这一矛盾需要解释。

Method: 基于Bethe ansatz分析低能激发；通过添加更高守恒荷并将链调至临界点，构造保持可积性的标度极限；该极限下能隙以二次方式闭合，需用非相对论场论描述，并给出其两粒子散射矩阵。

Result: 低能激发可解释为去禁闭畴壁（与sigma模型中相同）；能隙呈二次闭合，标度极限对应非相对论可积场论而非相对论$\mathbb{CP}^2$模型；导出该场论的两粒子S矩阵。

Conclusion: 晶格模型与场论的可积性矛盾源于相对论性假设；正确的低能描述是与$\mathbb{CP}^2$模型共享低能极限但非洛伦兹不变的可积场论。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic biquadratic spin-1 chain is gapped, $SU(3)$ symmetric, and integrable. Standard arguments suggest that its low-energy physics is described by the $\mathbb{CP}^2$ nonlinear sigma model at $θ=π$. Using the Bethe ansatz, we show that its low-energy excitations can be interpreted as deconfined domain walls, just as in the sigma model. However, the lattice model is integrable, whereas the field theory is generally believed not to be. We propose a solution to this mystery by finding an integrable field theory with the same low-energy limit but which is not Lorentz invariant. The integrability-preserving scaling limit is obtained by adding a higher conserved charge and tuning the chain to criticality. The gap closes quadratically, so that the limit must be described by some nonrelativistic field theory rather than the relativistic sigma model. We describe the two-particle scattering matrix of this field theory.

</details>
