### [105] [Genuinely Out-of-Equilibrium Bose-Einstein Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26123)
*Tatsuhiko N. Ikeda,Masahito Ueda*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文建立驱动-耗散的激子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚理论框架，证明在一、二维强激发下可形成凝聚体，三维下给出百分比量级的凝聚分数，与Morita等人实验量级一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子的基态是真空，因此其玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚只能在非平衡条件下发生，且必须对抗快速衰减，这构成理论处理上的核心困难。

Method: 构建驱动-耗散（driven-dissipative）理论框架，在一维和二维下考察强激发条件下的凝聚行为；在三维情形中引入有效损耗率与指定的注入能量进行计算。

Result: 在一维与二维中，足够强的激发可产生玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体；三维情形得到百分比量级的凝聚分数，其数量级与Morita等人（Nat. Commun. 13, 5388 (2022)）的激子实验结果相符。

Conclusion: 驱动-耗散框架可统一描述激子非平衡凝聚，预测低维激子凝聚及三维百分比量级凝聚分数，为相关实验提供理论支持。

Abstract: Bose-Einstein (BE) condensation typically occurs in the ground state. Prominent exceptions include excitons, whose ground state is the vacuum; consequently, they undergo BE condensation only in out-of-equilibrium regimes to counteract rapid decay. Here, we develop a driven-dissipative framework to address this challenge, demonstrating that BE condensates emerge under sufficiently strong excitation in one and two dimensions. In three dimensions, by incorporating effective loss rates and a specified injection energy, we find a percent-level condensate fraction, consistent in order of magnitude with the exciton experiment of Morita et al. (Nat. Commun. 13, 5388 (2022)).

</details>


### [106] [Determining quantum phases of eight-fold quasicrystal optical lattices with angular structure factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26320)
*Kohenjit Pebam,Deepak Gaur,Laxmi Angom,Bhumika Thoudam,Momin Hrangbung,Bikramjit Shoibam,D. Angom*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 用准晶扩展Bose-Hubbard模型与平均场方法研究Ammann-Beenker准晶光晶格中超冷原子的基态相，并发展结构因子计算方法以区分密度波与超固态相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶具有独特几何性质，是探索奇异物态的潜在平台；作者希望刻画准晶光晶格中超冷原子量子相的旋转对称性及性质。

Method: 以准晶扩展Bose-Hubbard模型（qeBHM）描述Ammann-Beenker准晶光晶格中的超冷原子，在软核（soft-core）区间用平均场方法确定基态；并建立计算态结构因子的理论方法以考察对角长程序。

Result: 所发展的结构因子计算方法能够清晰区分准晶中的多种密度波（DW）相与超固态（SS）相。

Conclusion: 平均场+结构因子分析可系统识别Ammann-Beenker准晶光晶格中的基态量子相及其旋转对称性，为准晶中奇异物态研究提供可行途径。

Abstract: Quasicrystals have unique geometrical properties and are potential candidates to explore exotic states of matter. In this work, we explore the properties and the rotational symmetries of the quantum phases of ultracold atoms in the Ammann-Beenker quasicrystal optical lattice. We model the system with the quasicrystalline extended Bose-Hubbard model (qeBHM) and use mean field methods to identify the groundstate of qeBHM in the soft-core regime. To examine the diagonal long-range order of the groundstate quantum phases we develop a theoretical method to calculate the structure factor of the state. Our method clearly distinguishes various density wave (DW) and supersolid (SS) phases of the quasicrystal.

</details>


### [107] [Are superfluidity and superconductivity related to the spontaneous breaking of $U(1)$ symmetry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26360)
*Maksim Tomchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 作者以严格的SSB判据（哈密顿量与边界条件在对称群变换下不变、而基态不变）分析有限体系，指出真实世界均为有限体系，故全局U(1)对称性不会自发破缺；因此超流与超导只能在无限体系中与SSB相联系，而无限体系在宇宙中并不存在，并讨论了SSB理论与现实的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为超流与超导源于全局U(1)规范/整体对称性的自发破缺，但真实物理系统总是有限的，有限体系能否真正实现SSB存在概念上的疑问，作者希望厘清SSB这一概念与物理现实之间的关系。

Method: 概念性/解析论证：采用严格的SSB判据——系统哈密顿量与边界条件在某一对称群的变换下保持不变，而基态在该变换下发生改变；据此对有限量子力学体系进行分析，并对照热力学极限（无限体系）的情形。

Result: 简单分析表明，在有限（即真实）系统中，全局U(1)对称性的自发破缺不会发生；超流/超导与SSB的对应关系仅在无限体系中成立。

Conclusion: SSB严格意义上只是无限体系的极限性质，并非真实有限系统的性质；将超流与超导归因于U(1)自发破缺本质上是一种理想化描述，需要重新审视SSB理论与物理现实之间关系的表述。

Abstract: It is widely believed that superfluidity and superconductivity are caused by the spontaneous breaking of global $U(1)$ symmetry. We investigate this question for quantum-mechanical systems using the following precise criterion for spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB): the system's Hamiltonian and boundary conditions are invariant under transformations of a certain symmetry group, while the ground state is not. Through a simple analysis, we show that in real-world systems, which are always finite, spontaneous breaking of $U(1)$ symmetry does not occur. Therefore, superfluidity and superconductivity can be related to SSB only in infinite systems, which do not exist in our Universe. We also discuss the relationship between the theory of SSB and reality.

</details>


### [108] [Local velocimetry for atomic superfluids: from Riemann rarefaction waves to quantized vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26608)
*Yansheng Zhang,Yi Jiang,Feiyang Wang,Louis Depaepe,J. Q. Matthew Chia,Christoph Eigen,Gehrig Carlse,Zoran Hadzibabic*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用基于拉曼-布拉格分束器的物质波干涉术，将超流波函数相位的局域梯度映射为干涉条纹位移，实现二维原子超流的局域测速，并重构出二维速度场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维原子超流缺乏直接、局域的速度场测量手段，而相位梯度即速度场这一核心关系难以在空间上分辨地提取。

Method: 基于拉曼-布拉格分束器的物质波干涉测量；把超流波函数相位的局域梯度映射到干涉条纹位移，进而重建二维速度场；应用于两个典型问题：一维黎曼稀疏波流的自相似速度场演化，以及量子化涡旋周围完整二维速度场的测绘。

Result: 直接观测到一维黎曼稀疏流中速度场的自相似演化；完整测绘出量子化涡旋周围的二维速度场，并明确验证了速度环流的量子化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了二维原子超流的局域速度场成像方法，为研究超流流体动力学（如稀疏波、涡旋动力学与环流量子化）提供直接的空间分辨探测工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate local velocimetry for atomic superfluids in two dimensions (2D). Using matter-wave interferometry based on Raman--Bragg beam splitters, we map the local gradient of the phase of the superfluid's wavefunction onto displacement of the interference fringes and reconstruct the 2D velocity field. We illustrate the capabilities of this technique by applying it to two canonical problems. First, we directly observe the self-similar evolution of the velocity field in 1D Riemann rarefaction flows. Second, we map out the full 2D velocity field around quantized vortices, explicitly demonstrating the quantization of velocity circulation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Exact Fractional Chern Insulators from Hubbard Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25010)
*Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Rui-Heng Liu,Jiangping Hu,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在紧束缚模型中构造了分数量子陈绝缘体的"精确配对"：多体波函数与其母体Hubbard相互作用哈密顿量，推广了Laughlin波函数与Haldane赝势这一分数量子霍尔效应中的经典配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laughlin波函数与Haldane赝势构成"精确配对"——波函数是无阻挫母体哈密顿量的唯一零能基态，这是理解分数量子霍尔效应的解析基础。但分数量子陈绝缘体（FCI）领域缺乏类似的解析可控配对，且此前普遍认为FCI基态只在"理想陈带"中存在。作者希望突破理想带的限制。

Method: 核心构造是引入"朗道对偶陈带"（Landau-dual Chern band）：从自旋-s狄拉克朗道能级出发，在Bravais格子上直接采样，得到一个多轨道陈带。多体波函数以矩阵坐标 X+iY+J 解析给出，其中 (X,Y) 为格点坐标，J 为自旋空间中的幂零矩阵。母体哈密顿量取为紧束缚模型加在位Hubbard相互作用。

Result: 所构造的精确配对覆盖了Laughlin、Read-Rezayi及Halperin系列的分数量子陈态，均为母体Hubbard哈密顿量的精确零能基态；由此证明FCI基态的存在不依赖于理想陈带，可推广到更一般的非理想带结构。

Conclusion: FCI基态存在于理想带之外，母体Hubbard相互作用可作为解析可控的出发哈密顿量；矩阵坐标中幂零矩阵J的引入提供了统一描述Laughlin/Read-Rezayi/Halperin态的新框架。

Abstract: Laughlin's wavefunction and its parent interaction, Haldane's pseudopotential, underpin our analytic understanding of fractional quantum Hall effect. They are an exact pair in the sense that the wavefunction is the unique, zero-energy ground state of its frustration-free parent Hamiltonian. In this work, we propose exact pairs of many-body wavefunctions for fractional Chern insulators in tight binding models with onsite Hubbard interactions as their parents. The key to our construction is introduction of a Landau-dual Chern band, a multi-orbital Chern band obtained from directly sampling the spin-$s$ Dirac Landau level on a Bravais lattice. The exact many-body wavefunctions are given, analytically, in terms of the matrix coordinate $X+iY+J$, where $(X,Y)$ are the lattice coordinates and $J$ is a nilpotent matrix in spin space. The constructed exact pairs cover the fractional Chern states of the Laughlin, the Read-Rezayi, and the Halperin series. Our results prove that FCI ground states exist beyond ideal bands.

</details>


### [110] [Anyon Coherent States in Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25034)
*Pedro Fittipaldi de Castro,Haylen Gerhard,Wladimir A. Benalcazar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于算符的系统化构造，给出分数量子霍尔体系与分数量子陈绝缘体中任意子（任意电荷）的最大局域化波函数，并证明其为饱和投影密度不确定性下界的局域湮灭算符零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔体系中描述任意子动力学、相互作用与编织需依赖相干任意子激发，但除Laughlin准空穴外，尚缺乏任意子波函数的一般表达式。

Method: 采用基于算符的系统化构造方法，构建分数量子霍尔态与分数量子陈绝缘体（含非理想、非涡旋化能带）中任意子的最大局域化波函数；以投影密度不确定性下界与局域湮灭算符零模刻画其相干性；通过任意子能带的拓扑性质与Bloch系数绕数分析其陈数。

Result: 所构造波函数为局域湮灭算符的零模，饱和投影密度不确定性下界，表现为相干准粒子；其存在源于任意子能带的非平凡拓扑，Bloch系数的绕数编码该能带的陈数；引导中心可位于晶格任意位置，相互重叠按由任意子能带有效磁长度决定的高斯形式衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作给出适用于任意电荷、任意拓扑带（含非涡旋化能带）的任意子相干态波函数的一般构造框架，将任意子相干性与能带拓扑（陈数）及有效磁长度定量联系。

Abstract: In fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems, coherent anyonic excitations are the natural entities for describing anyon dynamics, their interactions, and braiding, yet no general expression for their wavefunctions is known beyond Laughlin's quasiholes. We introduce a systematic, operator-based construction of maximally-localized wavefunctions of anyons of either charge in FQH states and fractional Chern insulators, including those in non-ideal, non-vortexable bands. We find that these wavefunctions describe coherent quasiparticles: zero modes of local annihilation operators that saturate projected-density uncertainty bounds. These coherent states owe their existence to a nontrivial topology of the anyon band, with the winding of their Bloch coefficients encoding an anyon band's Chern number. As such, their guiding centers exist anywhere within the lattice, and their mutual overlaps decay as Gaussians determined by the effective magnetic length of the anyon band.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum many-body scars and zero modes in the spin-1 Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25051)
*Ayush Sharma,Jay Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在笛卡尔基下分析自旋-1 Kitaev 链的希尔伯特空间连通性，证明手征对称性保护的精确零能模数目指数增长（周期偶数链下界为 Jacobsthal 数 (2^{L+1}+1)/3），并用算子-态映射解析构造出 2^{L/2} 个零模；进一步揭示嵌入的紧束缚结构导致多扇区的 PXP 型量子多体疤痕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1 Kitaev 链中的零能模与量子多体疤痕（QMBS）此前缺乏系统的代数刻画，尤其是零模数目的精确下界、可解析构造的巨量零模，以及疤痕存在的扇区范围与统一描述尚不清楚。

Method: （1）在笛卡尔基下分析希尔伯特空间连通性，结合手征对称性给出零能模数目的组合下界；（2）利用算子-态映射（operator-state mapping）解析构造零模并计算其连续二分割下的约化密度矩阵；（3）识别缺陷动力学所服从的嵌入紧束缚结构，导出前向散射近似（FSA）哈密顿量，并借助"缺陷在壁间传播"的图像刻画保真度复苏。

Result: 周期偶数长链的零能模数下界为 Jacobsthal 数 (2^{L+1}+1)/3；解析构造出 2^{L/2} 个零模，其对连续二分割具有极大混合密度矩阵；发现多个此前未报道的扇区存在 PXP 型 QMBS，且各疤痕扇区的 FSA 哈密顿量取统一形式。

Conclusion: 自旋-1 Kitaev 链的零模与疤痕结构由希尔伯特空间连通性与嵌入紧束缚图像统一支配；"缺陷在壁间传播"提供了识别保真度复苏扇区的直观结构判据，而其偏离之处则暗示存在额外的疤痕机制。

Abstract: We study the spin-1 Kitaev chain and, by analyzing its Hilbert-space connectivity in the Cartesian basis, show that it hosts an exponentially large number of exact zero-energy modes protected by chiral symmetry. For even-length periodic chains, their count is lower-bounded by the Jacobsthal number $(2^{L+1}+1)/3$, where $L$ is the chain length. We then construct an exponentially large number ($2^{L/2}$) of zero modes with an analytic expression that have a maximally mixed density matrix for a contiguous bipartition using the operator-state mapping. We further identify an embedded tight-binding structure governing defect dynamics that supports PXP-like quantum many-body scars (QMBS) in multiple sectors previously not reported. The associated forward-scattering approximation (FSA) Hamiltonian takes a universal form across the scarred sectors studied. A simple physical picture of defects propagating between walls provides an intuitive description of these dynamics and a structural guide to identifying sectors with fidelity revivals, while its limitations point to additional scarring mechanisms.

</details>


### [112] [The phase diagram of fluid hydrogen near melting from machine-learned interatomic potentials trained on quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25178)
*Shubhang Goswami,Hongwei Niu,Ilnur Saitov,Mathieu Istas,Yubo Yang,Scott Jensen,Markus Holzmann,Carlo Pierleoni,David Ceperley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 用两种基于量子蒙特卡洛训练的机器学习势进行路径积分分子动力学模拟，构建致密氢相图，发现分子-原子液体的第一性液-液一级相变终止于临界点，且三相点位于熔化曲线最高温处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密氢在固相熔融温度以上的相图（分子-原子解离是渐变还是突变）实验与理论均未精确确定，需要高精度方法厘清液-液相变与临界点、三相点位置。

Method: 采用两种机器学习原子间势（均由量子蒙特卡洛能量与力训练），结合经典与量子路径积分分子动力学模拟致密氢；用有限尺寸标定技术确定临界点，用最新技术构建相图。

Result: 两种模型均显示由分子液体到原子液体的第一性相变，终止于有限尺寸标定确定的临界点；液-液线与熔化曲线的交点给出位于熔化曲线最高温处的三相点，并预测斜坡压缩实验中应有清晰特征。

Conclusion: 致密氢液-液相变为一级相变并具临界终点，三相点位置（熔化曲线最高温处）应在斜坡压缩实验中产生可观测特征，为实验验证提供理论依据。

Abstract: Dissociation of molecular hydrogen increasing temperature or pressure may either occur gradually or abruptly, via a phase transition. Despite considerable experimental and theoretical efforts, the phase diagram of dense hydrogen at temperatures above solid melting is not precisely known. Here, we perform classical and quantum Path Integral molecular dynamics simulations of dense hydrogen using two machine-learned interatomic potentials both trained on quantum Monte Carlo energies and forces. The resulting phase diagram is constructed using state-of-the-art techniques. Both models show a first-order phase transition from the molecular to the atomic liquid terminating in critical points whose locations were determined using finite-size scaling techniques. Triple points, located at the highest temperature of the melting curve, are found at the intersection of the liquid-liquid lines with the melting curve and should lead to clear signatures in ramp compression experiments.

</details>


### [113] [Phase diagram of the attractive lattice Fermi polaron and the instability of Nagaoka ferromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26304)
*Gerard Pascual,Kris Van Houcke*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在方晶格低填充下，基态随相互作用依次由极化子经二聚子转为Yang η二聚子，超过三相点后直接由极化子转为η二聚子；填充率超过25%时始终为极化子；由于η二聚子是精确本征态，其边界给出Nagaoka铁磁性的严格失稳判据，比此前最佳判据更锐利且在低填充下呈对数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为方晶格上的吸引型极化子只与二聚子（dimeron）竞争，但存在被忽视的竞争态——Yang η二聚子（精确本征态，可映射到Nagaoka铁磁体）。需要厘清二者竞争对基态相图的影响，并借此获得Nagaoka铁磁性更严格的失稳判据。

Method: 采用两粒子–空穴（two-particle–hole）试探波函数（Ansatz），并以图解蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic Monte Carlo）作为基准进行标定与检验。

Result: 低填充下基态随相互作用由极化子→二聚子→η二聚子演化；存在三相点，超过该点后基态直接从极化子跃迁到η二聚子；填充率高于25%时基态始终保持为极化子。η二聚子边界在低填充下呈对数依赖，且比此前最佳判据更锐利。

Conclusion: η二聚子是精确本征态，因此其存在边界构成Nagaoka铁磁性的严格失稳判据，且在低填充极限下呈对数形式，优于以往判据；该工作给出了方晶格上吸引极化子–二聚子–η二聚子竞争的完整基态相图。

Abstract: On the square lattice, the attractive polaron competes not only with the dimeron but also with Yang's $η$ dimer, an exact eigenstate that maps onto the Nagaoka ferromagnet. With two-particle--hole Ansätze benchmarked by diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we find that the ground state evolves from polaron to dimeron to $η$ dimer at low filling, and, beyond a triple point, directly from polaron to $η$ dimer. Above 25\% filling it stays a polaron. As the $η$ dimer is exact, its boundary is a rigorous instability criterion for Nagaoka ferromagnetism, sharper than the previous best and logarithmic at low filling.

</details>


### [114] [Canted antiferromagnetism and spin-flop transition in honeycomb $α$-Na$_2$PrO$_3$ revealed by angle-dependent torque magnetometry up to 35 T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25291)
*Kylie MacFarquharson,Ryutaro Okuma,Matthew Pearce,Amalia I. Coldea,Alix McCollam,Roos Leenen,Uli Zeitler,Yurii Skourski,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过对α-Na2PrO3单晶的角度依赖压电悬臂扭矩测量，确定了零场下矩近垂直于蜂窝层的倾斜反铁磁结构，并用含易轴交换各向异性与DM耦合的最小自旋哈密顿量解释了角度依赖扭矩与场致自旋翻转行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-Na2PrO3被提出由自旋-轨道纠缠的赝自旋1/2 Pr4+间各向异性交换稳定非常规磁性，此前粉末磁化率仅显示低温反铁磁序及弱铁磁矩迹象，缺乏对磁结构取向与各向异性交换细节的直接测定。

Method: 对单晶在三个正交晶面进行角度依赖压电悬臂扭矩测量（磁场最高35 T，近垂直于层方向），结合平均场计算，构建含易轴交换各向异性与对称性允许的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合的最小自旋哈密顿量。

Result: 推断出零场倾斜反铁磁结构：矩近乎垂直于蜂窝层，弱倾斜产生沿面内单斜a轴的自发磁化，存在时间反演相关的两个磁畴；当沿a方向的场投影变号时扭矩出现尖锐跳变，解释为两简并畴的场致切换。近垂直场下35 T内观测到强异常，归因于接近预测的自旋翻转转变。DM相互作用将部分转变变为平滑渡越，并稳定一条连接垂直场自旋翻转与面内（垂直于a）场饱和的相界线，该相界线在无DM时不存在。

Conclusion: 扭矩测量结合最小模型定量刻画了α-Na2PrO3的倾斜反铁磁基态与场致相行为，表明DM相互作用对相图拓扑（相界线存在性与转变性质）具有决定性作用，为检验各向异性交换主导的赝自旋物理提供了判据。

Abstract: We report angle-dependent piezocantilever torque measurements on single crystals of the honeycomb magnet $α$-Na$_2$PrO$_3$, proposed to display unconventional magnetism stabilized by anisotropic exchange interactions between spin-orbit entangled, pseudospin 1/2 Pr$^{4+}$ magnetic moments. Previous powder susceptibility observed signatures of a transition at low temperatures to antiferromagnetic order with a weak ferromagnetic moment. From the torque angular dependence in three orthogonal crystallographic planes, we infer a zero-field canted antiferromagnetic structure, with moments almost normal to the honeycomb layers, but weakly canted such as to give rise to a spontaneous magnetization along the in-plane monoclinic $a$-axis, with two magnetic domains related by time reversal. Sharp jumps in the torque angular dependence when the field projection along $a$ changes sign are interpreted as field-induced switching between the two degenerate domains with oppositely aligned ferromagnetic moments. We propose a minimal spin Hamiltonian including easy-axis exchange anisotropy and symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) couplings, for which mean-field calculations reproduce the main features of the observed angle-dependent torque. Torque measurements up to 35 T applied almost normal to the layers observe a strong anomaly attributed to proximity to the field-induced spin-flop transition predicted for the above Hamiltonian. We contrast the predicted magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic fields in different orthogonal crystallographic planes normal to the honeycomb layers and discuss how DM interactions convert some transitions into smooth crossovers and stabilize a phase boundary line connecting the spin-flop transition in field normal to the layers to magnetization saturation for in-plane field normal to $a$; such a phase boundary line does not exist in the absence of DM interactions.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Spin Hamiltonians from Interacting Magnon Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25321)
*Ang-Kun Wu,David A. Dahlbom,Kipton Barros,Cristian D. Batista,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出用快速多体求解器（截断希尔伯特空间阶梯法，THL）生成训练数据训练神经网络，从中子散射谱反演各向异性三角晶格自旋哈密顿量参数，克服线性自旋波理论的系统性偏差；一维卷积网络表现最佳，并可用学习到的正向解码器做自洽检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络学习逆映射时会继承训练数据所用正向求解器的近似误差。以LSWT作为正向模型无法刻画磁振子相互作用导致的谱移与展宽，从而给哈密顿量参数反演带来系统偏差，需要更精确又快得足以生成大规模训练集的求解器。

Method: 针对场略高于饱和场的各向异性三角晶格自旋哈密顿量族，用THL方法在一、二磁振子空间内非微扰计算动力学自旋结构因子（DSSF），生成大规模合成中子散射谱训练集；训练多种神经网络架构做参数回归（一维CNN最优）；再用学习到的正向解码器由预测参数重建DSSF作为自洽检查与可视化；并通过Fisher信息矩阵分析测量协议（可分辨的自旋关联分量、动量截断）对可辨识耦合的限制。

Result: 1D卷积网络在参数预测上优于其他架构；相互作用强时，分别以LSWT谱与THL谱训练的推断参数差异显著；Fisher信息矩阵分析表明测量协议中的自旋关联分量选择与动量截断决定了哪些耦合可被识别。

Conclusion: 逆映射网络受其正向求解器精度的制约，将机器学习与快速多体理论结合，是实现散射数据定量哈密顿量反演的必要条件。

Abstract: A neural network that learns an inverse map inherits the approximations of the forward solver used to generate its training data. We demonstrate this in the inference of spin Hamiltonians from synthetic neutron-scattering spectra and show how a fast many-body solver can overcome the limitation of linear spin-wave theory (LSWT). We consider a family of anisotropic triangular-lattice spin Hamiltonians at fields just above saturation, where magnon interactions shift and broaden the spectrum in ways that LSWT cannot capture. Using the truncated Hilbert-space ladder (THL) method, we compute the dynamical spin structure factor (DSSF) nonperturbatively within the one- and two-magnon space. THL is fast enough to generate large training sets of DSSF spectra, enabling us to train neural networks that accurately predict Hamiltonian parameters. By empirically benchmarking several neural-network architectures, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional network performs best. A learned forward decoder then reconstructs the DSSF from the predicted parameters, providing a rapid self-consistency check and a visualization of the predicted response. When interactions are strong, networks trained on LSWT and THL spectra infer markedly different parameters. Analysis of the Fisher information matrix shows that the measurement protocol, including which spin-correlation components are resolved and the momentum cut, determines which couplings can be identified. Combining machine learning with fast many- body theory is therefore essential for quantitative Hamiltonian inference from scattering data.

</details>


### [116] [Symmetry-resolved spectroscopy of truncation-induced perturbations in loop tensor network renormalization of the six-vertex model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25322)
*Akira Otsuki,Yuta Yamamoto,Kokin Cho,Rikuto Fushio,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文从对称性分辨的转移矩阵谱与共形微扰论出发，逐算符识别 LoopTNR 在有限键维 χ 下的截断误差，并定量验证其增长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维张量网络重整化中的截断误差会被RG流放大，但具体由哪些算符主导、增长是否可由共形微扰论定量描述，此前缺乏逐算符的清晰诊断。

Method: 在六顶角模型 c=1 临界线上（Luttinger 参数 K），对 LoopTNR 使用对称性分辨的转移矩阵谱确定误差算符；再用共形微扰论计算其每步增长与振幅比值，与数值结果对比；并考察不同对称性约束与不同 χ。

Result: 施加 O(2) 对称性时主导误差为二聚化算符（因 LoopTNR 破坏一格平移不变性而允许存在）；对称性更少时为 U(1) 流或荷二顶点算符。二聚化的每RG步增长及无量纲振幅比与共形微扰论吻合至百分之几。K 因弱边缘误差及二聚化引起的跑动而缓慢漂移。增大 χ 在 0.75≤K≤1.5 内降低误差初始振幅，但不改变误差算符种类与增长率。

Conclusion: LoopTNR 的有限χ误差可被逐算符归因并定量由共形微扰论描述；增大键维只压制初始振幅而非增长指数，故误差放大源于RG流本身而非截断幅度。

Abstract: Tensor network renormalization at finite bond dimension $χ$ introduces truncation errors that are amplified by the renormalization-group (RG) flow. For loop tensor network renormalization (LoopTNR) on the $c=1$ critical line of the six-vertex model, with Luttinger parameter $K$, we identify these errors operator by operator from symmetry-resolved transfer-matrix spectra and conformal perturbation theory. With $O(2)$ symmetry imposed, the dominant error is the dimerization operator, which is allowed because LoopTNR breaks one-site translation. With less symmetry it is the $U(1)$ current or a charge-two vertex operator. The growth of the dimerization per RG step and a parameter-free ratio of its amplitudes agree with conformal perturbation theory to within a few percent. The parameter $K$ drifts slowly, owing to a weak marginal error and to the running induced by the dimerization. A larger $χ$ lowers the initial amplitude of the error for $0.75\le K\le 1.5$ but changes neither the operator nor its growth rate.

</details>


### [117] [Momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo for twisted bilayer graphene: Interaction effects in the presence of remote bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25522)
*Shibo Shan,Cheng Huang,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将动量空间量子蒙特卡洛扩展到显式包含远程BM能带，验证了平带投影在u0/u1≲0.9范围内的可靠性，并指出近带接触极限下远程带自由度不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影模型中相互作用诱导的Mott能隙可与无相互作用的平带—远程带间距相比拟，这使"仅投影到中心平带"的合法性受到质疑，需要定量检验投影近似的适用范围，并调和投影观点与THF（拓扑重费米子/平带—远程带耦合）观点之间的分歧。

Method: 在电荷中性点，将动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）扩展为在较宽能量窗口内显式包含远程BM（Bistritzer–MacDonald）能带，并与仅投影到中心平带的计算在同一参数下对比，考察低能电子谱、三重子（trion）谱以及Kramers谷间相干基态的稳定性。

Result: 在u0/u1≲0.9的宽广参数区内，投影与含远程带的结果高度一致：低能电子谱与trion谱相似，Kramers谷间相干基态保持稳定，有限温正常态中的Dirac trion在包含远程带时依然稳健。接近带接触极限u0=u1时，远程带电子获得显著低能谱权重，trion权重被抑制，Kramers谷间相干态消失。

Conclusion: 平带投影在大多数参数区具有广泛有效性，两种观点可在"近带接触区域需显式包含远程带自由度"这一认识下统一。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene hosts strong correlation effects within topological flat bands separated from remote dispersive bands. This separation motivates projection onto the central bands, which greatly reduces the computational complexity of many-body calculations, including momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations. However, the interaction-induced Mott gap obtained in the projected model can be comparable to the noninteracting flat--remote band separation, raising the question of whether the projection remains valid. Here, we extend momentum-space QMC to explicitly include remote BM bands within a broad energy window at charge neutrality. We find that the projected description remains reliable over a broad parameter regime, up to $u_0/u_1\lesssim0.9$: the projected and remote-band calculations yield similar low-energy electron and trion spectra, and the Kramers intervalley-coherent ground state remains stable. In particular, the Dirac trion remains robust in the finite-temperature normal state when remote bands are included. Near the band-touching limit $u_0=u_1$, remote-band electrons acquire substantial low-energy spectral weight, accompanied by suppression of the trion weight and loss of the Kramers intervalley-coherent state. Our findings establish the broad validity of flat-band projection while reconciling the projected and THF viewpoints by identifying the near-band-touching regime in which explicit remote-band degrees of freedom become essential.

</details>


### [118] [Interaction-driven phase transition between bosonic Moore-Read and Laughlin states in a multiple flat-band model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25633)
*Licheng Wang,Jia-Yuan Wu,Dong-Hao Guan,Ai-Lei He,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在含两个低能近平带的六格点Kekulé晶格模型中，通过实空间与能带投影精确对角化及iDMRG，给出了固定填充下摩尔-里德（非阿贝尔）与Laughlin型（阿贝尔）拓扑序之间相互作用驱动相变的数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重近平带晶格模型为实现分数量子陈绝缘体、研究不同拓扑序之间的竞争提供了额外自由度；作者希望在同一填充因子下寻找非阿贝尔与阿贝尔拓扑序之间的相变，并突破传统多层体系的平台限制。

Method: 构建六格点Kekulé晶格模型，其两个低能近平带的陈数分别为1和0；在每元胞一个玻色子（格点填充1/6）下施加两体排斥，结合实空间与能带投影精确对角化，并用低能谱、粒子纠缠谱、多体陈数进行态识别，同时辅以无限密度矩阵重整化群（iDMRG）计算。

Result: 弱两体排斥时体系倾向于主要占据最低能带的Moore-Read态；较强排斥时则形成同时显著占据两个低能带的Laughlin型态。低能谱、粒子纠缠谱与多体陈数一致地确认了这两种拓扑序及其间的相变。

Conclusion: 该多重近平带Kekulé模型为研究不同拓扑序间的量子相变提供了不同于传统多层体系的新平台，表明多近平带自由度可用于按相互作用强度调控非阿贝尔/阿贝尔拓扑序。

Abstract: Lattice models with multiple nearly flat bands provide additional degrees of freedom for realizing fractional Chern insulators and studying competition between distinct topological orders. Here we report numerical evidence for the interaction-driven phase transition between the non-Abelian and Abelian topologically ordered states at fixed filling factor in a multiple flat-band lattice model. We construct a six-site Kekulé lattice model with two low-energy nearly flat bands whose Chern numbers are $1$ and $0$, respectively. At one boson per unit cell (a site filling of $\frac{1}{6}$), the weak two-body repulsion favors a Moore-Read state predominantly occupying the lowest band, whereas stronger repulsion favors a Laughlin-like state with substantial occupations of both lower bands. We identify the two states by the low-energy spectra, particle entanglement spectra, and many-body Chern numbers, using the real-space and band-projected exact diagonalization, supplemented by infinite density-matrix renormalization group calculations. Our results provide alternative platform to study the quantum phase transition of distinct topological orders beyond the conventional multilayer systems.

</details>


### [119] [Current-driven resistive switching in high-field heavy fermion metal UTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25792)
*Ling Zhang,Chunyu,Guo,David Graf,Carsten Putzke,Mitchell M. Bordelon,Eric D. Bauer,Sean M. Thomas,Filip Ronning,Priscila F. S. Rosa,Philip J. W. Moll*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过FIB微结构在UTe₂中达到30–60 kA/cm²电流密度，在PM与FP两个相区均观察到临界电流密度约10 kA/cm²以上的电流诱导高阻态，且临界电流密度随温度升高而增大，排除焦耳热机制，揭示两相共有同一不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂中70 T高场超导的母态是场极化（FP）态，但FP态本身认识不足，尤其是它与低场顺磁（PM）态是否共享同一底层物理尚不清楚；需要一种新手段探测近邻电子态。

Method: 用聚焦离子束（FIB）制备微结构以承受高达30–60 kA/cm²的电流密度，在bc面内大范围磁场角度下对PM与FP两个相区进行电流输运测量。

Result: 临界电流密度约10 kA/cm²以上时电阻率陡升至与变磁转变临界终点附近相当的量级；临界电流密度随温度升高而增大，与焦耳热预期相反，排除热起源；PM与FP两侧出现相同的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 变磁转变两侧出现同一电流诱导不稳定性，说明FP与PM态具有出乎意料的共性；电流可像磁场、压力一样在重费米子金属的多个近邻电子态之间切换。

Abstract: In UTe$_2$, superconductivity persists up to 70 T for fields tilted by roughly 20-40° from the b axis toward c. Its normal state is a field-polarized (FP) state, which emerges above a first-order metamagnetic transition when the field component along b exceeds $\sim$35 T. Despite being the parent of this high-field superconductivity, the FP state remains poorly understood, including whether it shares underlying physics with the low-field paramagnetic (PM) state from which it is abruptly separated. Using microstructures fabricated by focused ion beam, we reach current densities of 30-60 kA/cm2 and observe a current-induced high-resistivity state in both the PM and FP regimes over a wide range of field angles in the bc plane. Above a critical current density of order 10 kA/cm2, the resistivity sharply rises to values comparable to those near the critical end point of the metamagnetic transition. The critical current density increases with temperature, opposite to the trend expected from Joule heating, ruling out a thermal origin. The emergence of the same instability on both sides of the metamagnetic transition reveals an unexpected commonality between the FP and PM states. More broadly, the observation of a current-induced transition in a heavy-fermion metal exposes the multitude of nearby electronic states in correlated electron systems and shows that electric current, like magnetic field and pressure, can switch between them.

</details>


### [120] [Fast extraction of universal data from temporal spectrum: Applications to Haagerup CFT and monopole scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26257)
*Tokiro Numasawa,Boris De Vos,Masataka Watanabe,Atsushi Ueda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种从无限矩阵乘积态转移矩阵谱中快速精确读取共形场论谱的算法，应用于Haagerup CFT得到中心荷 c=2.137(27)，并给出了Maldacena–Ludwig单极子边界的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上提取格点共形场论的普适数据需要大规模模拟临界体系的基态与激发态，代价高昂；作者希望找到一种更快、更准确的替代算法。

Method: 先做短时虚时演化，然后从无限矩阵乘积态（iMPS）的转移矩阵谱中读出CFT谱；将该方法用于提取Haagerup CFT的荷分辨谱至更高能级；并通过时空旋转，论证Maldacena–Ludwig单极子边界可表现为Fidkowski–Kitaev哈密顿量的基态。

Result: 获得Haagerup CFT的荷分辨谱更高能级数据，中心荷估计为 c = 2.137(27)；并展示Maldacena–Ludwig单极子边界可作为Fidkowski–Kitaev哈密顿量时空旋转后的基态实现。

Conclusion: 基于iMPS转移矩阵谱的方法为格点CFT普适数据（谱与中心荷）提供了一种高效、精确的提取途径，并揭示了单极子边界与Fidkowski–Kitaev模型之间的对偶联系。

Abstract: Extracting precise universal data on conformal field theory (CFT) on the lattice traditionally requires large-scale simulations of the ground states and excitations of critical systems. Here, we propose an alternative accurate and fast algorithm, where we read off the CFT spectrum from the transfer matrix spectrum of an infinite matrix product state after a short imaginary-time evolution. We apply this method to extract the charge-resolved spectrum of a Haagerup CFT to higher levels and obtain a central charge $c = 2.137(27)$. Moreover, we show that the Maldacena--Ludwig monopole boundary can be realized as the ground state of the Fidkowski--Kitaev Hamiltonian after space-time rotation.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Metric Control of Topological Superconductivity and Incommensurate Charge Density Wave in an Interacting Lieb-Kitaev Chain Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26327)
*Jiale Xuan,Grant Z. X. Yang,K. T. Law,Xiao Yan Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在近平坦带区研究相互作用无自旋Lieb-Kitaev链，通过调节相互作用强度与量子几何度规，识别出拓扑超导相、不可公度电荷密度波相与公度电荷密度波相，并发现量子度规增强量子度规长度、驱动不连续IC-C相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现非相互作用Lieb-Kitaev系统中马约拉纳零模继承两种衰减模式：普通BdG长度与由量子度规控制的更长几何长度，本文据此将研究推广至相互作用情形，考察量子几何度规对关联相与相变的调控作用。

Method: 构建相互作用无自旋Lieb-Kitaev链模型，聚焦近平坦带区域；同时调节相互作用强度与量子度规，采用低能有效理论与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值计算相互验证。

Result: 识别出拓扑超导相（TSC）、不可公度电荷密度波相（ICDW）与公度电荷密度波相（CDW）；在TSC相内，量子度规长度（QML）随相互作用增强而增大；在ICDW相中，相互作用受量子度规调制产生有效化学势，导致不连续的不可公度—公度（IC-C）相变。

Conclusion: 量子几何度规可作为调节相互作用体系相图与相变性质的有效手段，且低能有效理论与DMRG结果一致，支持所提出的相图与物理机制。

Abstract: We study an interacting spinless Lieb-Kitaev chain model in the nearly flat-band regime, motivated by the recent observation that Majorana zero modes in the noninteracting Lieb-Kitaev system inherit two decay modes: an ordinary Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) length and a longer geometric length controlled by the quantum metric. By tuning both the interaction strength and quantum metric, we identify a topological superconducting (TSC) phase, an incommensurate charge-density-wave (ICDW)phase, and a commensurate charge-density-wave (CDW) phase. Inside the TSC phase, the QML is enhanced when increasing the interactions. In the ICDW phase, the interactions are modulated by quantum metric to generate an effective chemical potential, leading to a discontinuous incommensurate-to-commensurate (IC-C) transition. We verify these phases and conclusions using low energy effective theory and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations.

</details>


### [122] [Nonstandard quantum statistics for strongly correlated systems: Superexclusion in momentum space and comparison with exact results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26489)
*Piotr Kuterba,Mateusz Wójcik,Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了强关联费米子的两类非标准统计——统计自旋液体(SSL)与模型非费米液体(NFL)，二者均以实空间或倒空间中完全排除局域双占位(超排除)为基础，并结合k空间Hubbard类模型的精确分析，讨论了统计向局域磁矩的嬗变、费米液体向强关联费米液体或Mott绝缘体的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子体系中常规费米液体图像失效，需要从统计(而非仅从相互作用)角度理解非费米液体与关联绝缘相的出现机制；作者希望以"超排除"(完全禁止局域双占位)为统一出发点，在实空间与k空间两种表示下建立可类比的非标准统计框架。

Method: 以"超排除"即完全排除局域双占位为核心假设，分别构造实空间(SSL)与倒空间k(NFL)两类统计；前者在纯粹物理图像下推导结果，后者借助k空间Hubbard类微观模型进行精确分析；并考察这两类统计向局域磁矩统计的嬗变、费米液体向强关联费米液体/半满Mott绝缘体的转变，以及玻色子—费米子统计嬗变。

Result: 双占位排除所在空间(实空间或k空间)决定了关联相的形成方式；费米液体可转变为强关联费米液体，在半满情形下转变为Mott绝缘体；在强排斥玻色子相互作用极限下，玻色子—费米子统计嬗变导致粒子局域化而非玻色—爱因斯坦凝聚。

Conclusion: 两类模型具有与实空间或k空间表示无关的普适特征；超排除是关联相出现的核心要素，统计嬗变(包括玻色子到费米子)是连接不同表示形式的统一机制。

Abstract: We analyze in detail nonstandard statistics for strongly correlated fermions, which comprise the statistical spin liquid (SSL) and model non-Fermi liquid (NFL) cases. Both of those cases are based on the total exclusion of local doubly occupancies ($the~superexclusion$) either in real space (SSL case) or in reciprocal ($\mathbf{k}$) space (NFL). The results obtained on a purely physical ground are supplemented with the exact analysis of those coming from microscopic Hubbard-like model in $\mathbf{k}$ space. Specifically, we discuss the transmutation of those statistics to that of localized magnetic moments, as well as the transformation of the Fermi liquid into a strongly correlated fermionic liquid or Mott insulating state; the latter taking place for the half-filled-band situation. The role of exclusion of double occupancies in either real or reciprocal space in the appearance of the correlated phase is stressed. Conclusions concerning the universal features of the models, independence of either the real- or $\mathbf{k}$-space representation, are also discussed. One of those is the boson to fermion statistics transmutation which in the limit of strong repulsive interboson interaction leads to the particle localization instead to the Bose-Einstein condensation.

</details>


### [123] [Magnon high-harmonic generation by nonlinearities of spin torque driven by phonon-mediated crossing of many-body energy levels of bright and dark excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26589)
*Jalil Varela-Manjarres,Federico Garcia-Gaitan,Branislav K. Nikolic*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出一个单一微观机制统一解释CrSBr中磁振子高次谐波产生与磁振子振荡持续约1 ns（远超飞秒泵浦脉宽）两种超快光驱现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrSBr作为二维反铁磁半导体在超快光激发下出现多种反常现象（磁振子高次谐波、振荡寿命远超泵浦脉宽），此前缺乏统一解释，作者希望用一个微观机制同时说明二者。

Method: 将磁振子视为系统，将光学亮/暗激子与声子视为量子多体热库，推导含热库贡献的扩展Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（eLLG）方程；热库项空间非局域、含时，并在声子介导的亮-暗激子多体能级交叉时呈强非线性。

Result: 新热库项形态类似自旋电子学中的场型自旋转移矩（STT），但由激子而非电流驱动，从而给出实验推测的“激子型STT”的微观起源；同时可解释磁振子高次谐波与长寿命振荡。

Conclusion: 暗激子在飞秒激光关闭后长时间向磁振子供能，构成可工程化的能量储库，为光自旋电子学、光磁子学、神经形态计算与量子换能等应用提供新途径。

Abstract: Recent intense experimental focus on CrSBr, a two-dimensional (2D) antiferromagnetic semiconductor, has revealed numerous surprises in its response to ultrafast optical drive. We demonstrate that a single microscopic mechanism accounts for two of them---high-harmonic generation of magnons and persistence of magnon oscillations over \mbox{$\sim 1$ ns} time scale that is much longer than the femtosecond laser pulse (fsLP) pump duration. This mechanism arises from an unusually complex interplay of four different fundamental quasiparticles of condensed matter physics: magnons, optically bright and dark excitons, and phonons. By viewing magnons as the system and the other three quasiparticles as a quantum many-body bath, we derive an extended Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (eLLG) equation. Its new term generated by the bath is spatially nonlocal and time-dependent, as well as strongly {\em nonlinear} when the {\em crossing of many-body energy levels} of bright and dark excitons occurs via phonon mediation. It also resembles the so-called field-like spin-transfer torque (STT) in spintronics, but STT is driven there with current pulses, so it explains the microscopic origin of the recently experimentally conjectured {\em excitonic STT}. Since dark excitons supply energy to magnons long after the fsLP is switched off, such a reservoir opens avenues for its engineering and applications in optospintronics and optomagnonics, neuromorphic computing, and quantum transduction.

</details>


### [124] [Scalable Simulation of Strongly Correlated Fermions with Neural Gaussian Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26604)
*Runze Chi,Ao Chen,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出神经网络高斯投影纠缠对态（NN-GPEPS），利用高斯态的局域纠缠子空间提升与神经网络的兼容性，在二维格子上实现 O(N^{3/2}) 的精确收缩代价；在 U/t=8、1/8 空穴掺杂的 Hubbard 模型上于最大 1024 格点得到最优变分能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子的精确模拟仍是计算物理的核心难题，需要兼具表达力与可扩展性的变分方法。

Method: 构建 NN-GPEPS 架构：以高斯态的局域纠缠子空间为出发点，使张量网络结构与现代神经网络更兼容，并利用该子空间性质将二维格子上的精确收缩复杂度降至 O(N^{3/2})。

Result: 在 Hubbard 模型 U/t=8、1/8 空穴掺杂条件下，于多达 1024 个格点的大规模体系获得当前最优（state-of-the-art）变分能量。

Conclusion: 将神经网络与高斯张量网络相连接，可为强关联费米子提供一种精确且可扩展的变分途径。

Abstract: Accurate simulations of strongly correlated electrons remain a central challenge in computational physics. We introduce neural-network Gaussian projected entangled-pair states (NN-GPEPS), which exploit the locally-entangled subspace of Gaussian states to give an architecture more compatible with modern neural networks and an exact contraction cost of $O(N^{3/2})$ on two-dimensional lattices. For the Hubbard model at $U/t=8$ and $1/8$ hole doping, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies in large lattices of up to 1024 sites. These results demonstrate an accurate approach to strongly correlated fermions by connecting neural networks with Gaussian tensor networks.

</details>
